/bin/bash -lc 'cat src/compiler/compile.js' in /workspace/cel-town
 succeeded in 0ms:
// Seeded town compiler (v2). compileTown(spec, catalog, {seed}) -> town, fully
// deterministic for a given spec+seed. The catalog is the RESOLVED Map
// (id -> resolved entry, world units) so this stays pure JS — no three.js, no
// DOM — and runs identically in node and the browser.
//
// v2 adds: a paved plaza RING every door path joins (no dead-ends), civic
// LANDMARKS placed on the best plaza-facing slots before houses, continuous
// per-lot FENCES with a gated front, DENSITY multipliers + area auto-grow, and
// a panel-aware camera.
//
// v3 adds: door FACING (yaw blended from plaza-facing toward the camera at the
// arc ends, capped by houses.facing; recorded per building as
// facing:{cameraAngle, plazaAngle}), door paths that run straight out of the
// door onto the ring (bend <= facing.maxPathBend), a GATE instance in every
// fenced lot's front gap, and a tighter fill-based camera fit.
import { makeRng, deriveSeed } from './rng.js';
import { math as Math } from './math.js';
import { applyDefaults, scaleCount, gateSpecError } from './spec.js';
import { validate } from './checks.js';
import {
  TAU, toWorldXZ, rotDir, obbFromRect, rectXZ, obbCorners, obbOverlap,
  obbPointDistance, segPointDistance, strutEnds, norm, pointInArea, dist,
} from './geometry.js';

// Camera lives on the +x/+z side (matches the default stage view); buildings go
// on the arc AWAY from it so their fronts face the viewer.
const VIEWER_DIR = norm([30, 34]);
const AWAY_ANGLE = Math.atan2(-VIEWER_DIR[1], -VIEWER_DIR[0]); // atan2(z, x)

// Instance kinds the camera must keep fully framed (dressing may crop).
export const CORE_KINDS = new Set(['house', 'landmark', 'well', 'ring', 'path', 'fence', 'gate', 'prop']);

// Left fraction of the frame reserved for the on-screen panel (compile page);
// the camera fit keeps the core clear of it.
const PANEL_LEFT_CUT = 0.22;
// Fraction of the available frame (right of the panel) the core should span
// on its binding axis.
const CAMERA_FILL = 0.88;
const CAMERA_ELEV_DEG = 37;
const CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG = 45; // the fit may raise the camera this far to use the frame height
const CAMERA_FOV = 42;
// First guess for the camera's ground position when choosing door yaws (the
// real camera is suggested from the finished layout; compileTown re-runs the
// layout against it when the guess was off).
const CAM_HINT_DIST = 38;
// Yaws aim this far inside maxCameraAngle (the hint camera is a guess).
const CAM_MARGIN = 3 / (180 / Math.PI);

const DEG = 180 / Math.PI;
const wrapAngle = (a) => Math.atan2(Math.sin(a), Math.cos(a));
/** Yaw (rotY convention: door +Z -> [sin, cos]) that points along [x, z]. */
const yawOf = (v) => Math.atan2(v[0], v[1]);

/**
 * Horizontal facing angles (degrees) of a building at `pos` with yaw `rotY`:
 * door direction vs the direction to the camera ground point / plaza centre.
 */
export function facingAngles(pos, rotY, camXZ) {
  const yaw = rotY;
  const toCam = yawOf([camXZ[0] - pos[0], camXZ[1] - pos[1]]);
  const toPlaza = yawOf([-pos[0], -pos[1]]);
  return {
    cameraAngle: +(Math.abs(wrapAngle(yaw - toCam)) * DEG).toFixed(2),
    plazaAngle: +(Math.abs(wrapAngle(yaw - toPlaza)) * DEG).toFixed(2),
  };
}

/**
 * Straight door path from a building's path snap toward the plaza ring.
 * Aims along the door direction and ends where that ray meets the circle of
 * radius `R` (just inside the ring centreline); if the ray misses, it bends
 * toward the tangent point. Returns { a, end, bend (radians), dir } or null.
 */
function doorPath(entry, pos, rotY, R) {
  const snap = entry.pathSnaps?.[0];
  if (!snap) return null;
  const a = toWorldXZ([snap.pos[0], snap.pos[2]], { x: pos[0], z: pos[1], rotY });
  const f = rotDir([0, 1], rotY);
  const ls = Math.hypot(a[0], a[1]);
  if (ls <= R + 0.5) return null;
  const b = a[0] * f[0] + a[1] * f[1];
  const disc = b * b - (ls * ls - R * R);
  if (disc >= 0 && -b - Math.sqrt(disc) > 0.5) {
    const t = -b - Math.sqrt(disc);
    return { a, end: [a[0] + f[0] * t, a[1] + f[1] * t], bend: 0, dir: f };
  }
  // Ray misses: aim at the tangent point on f's side.
  const yU = yawOf([-a[0], -a[1]]);
  const yF = rotY;
  const beta = Math.asin(R / ls);
  const yG = yU + Math.sign(wrapAngle(yF - yU) || 1) * beta;
  const g = [Math.sin(yG), Math.cos(yG)];
  const t = Math.sqrt(ls * ls - R * R);
  return { a, end: [a[0] + g[0] * t, a[1] + g[1] * t], bend: Math.abs(wrapAngle(yF - yG)), dir: g };
}

// Mirrors builders.js gateLeafLength (GATE_POST 0.26, GATE_LEAF_MAX 1.0; two
// leaves when > 2.2 wide) — kept here so the compiler stays three.js-free.
function gateLeafLength(width) {
  const span = width - 0.26 - 0.06;
  return Math.min(1.0, width > 2.2 ? span / 2 - 0.08 : span - 0.08);
}
const GATE_STOCK = 0.065; // leaf stock depth behind the hinge line (rails/brace)
/** Largest `open` whose deepest leaf point stays within `depth` of the hinge line. */
function gateOpenMax(width, depth) {
  const len = gateLeafLength(width);
  let lo = 0, hi = 1;
  if (len * 1 + 0 <= depth) return 1;
  for (let k = 0; k < 30; k++) {
    const m = (lo + hi) / 2, a = m * Math.PI / 2;
    if (len * Math.sin(a) + GATE_STOCK * Math.cos(a) <= depth) lo = m; else hi = m;
  }
  return lo;
}

/** Path strip instance fields (+ its OBB) from a door path. */
function pathStrip(dp, width) {
  const dx = dp.end[0] - dp.a[0], dz = dp.end[1] - dp.a[1];
  const length = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
  const mid = [(dp.a[0] + dp.end[0]) / 2, (dp.a[1] + dp.end[1]) / 2];
  const rotY = Math.atan2(dx, dz); // strip length runs along local Z
  const obb = obbFromRect({ min: [-width / 2, -length / 2], max: [width / 2, length / 2] }, { x: mid[0], z: mid[1], rotY });
  return { mid, rotY, length, obb };
}

// --- catalog tag queries ---------------------------------------------------
export function queryCatalog(catalog, pick = {}) {
  let list = [...catalog.values()];
  if (pick.category) list = list.filter((e) => e.tags.category === pick.category);
  if (pick.style) list = list.filter((e) => (e.tags.style || []).includes(pick.style));
  if (pick.source) list = list.filter((e) => e.source === pick.source);
  return list;
}

function pickEntry(rng, list, prefer) {
  if (!list.length) return null;
  if (prefer) {
    const pref = list.filter((e) => (!prefer.source || e.source === prefer.source)
      && (!prefer.style || (e.tags.style || []).includes(prefer.style)));
    // Favour preferred entries ~70% of the time when both exist.
    if (pref.length && (pref.length === list.length || rng.chance(0.7))) return rng.pick(pref);
  }
  return rng.pick(list);
}

function plazaCenterId(spec, catalog) {
  const c = spec.plaza.center;
  if (typeof c === 'string') return catalog.has(c) ? c : queryCatalog(catalog, { category: 'well' })[0]?.id;
  return queryCatalog(catalog, c)[0]?.id;
}

/** Local-rect for an instance's XZ extent: paths/rings are parametric. */
export function rectForInst(inst, catalog) {
  if (inst.kind === 'path') return { min: [-inst.params.width / 2, -inst.params.length / 2], max: [inst.params.width / 2, inst.params.length / 2] };
  if (inst.kind === 'ring') { const o = inst.params.outer; return { min: [-o, -o], max: [o, o] }; }
  const e = catalog.get(inst.id);
  if (inst.kind === 'gate' || e?.builder === 'makeGate') return gateRect(gateParams(inst, e));
  return rectXZ(e.bounds);
}

/** Effective makeGate params for an instance: instance fields > params > catalog. */
export function gateParams(inst, entry) {
  const p = { ...(entry?.params || {}), ...(inst.params || {}) };
  if (inst.width != null) p.width = inst.width;
  if (inst.open != null) p.open = inst.open;
  return { width: p.width ?? 2.9, open: p.open ?? 0.55, height: p.height ?? 1.22 };
}

/**
 * Conservative local XZ rect of makeGate({width, open}) (unscaled; obbFromRect
 * applies instance scale). Mirrors the builder: posts GATE_POST 0.26 with a
 * 0.36 cap at x = +-width/2; leaves of gateLeafLength(width) hinged just inside
 * the posts and swung open*90 deg toward -Z; leaf stock reaches -0.065..+0.045
 * in z before swinging. 0.05 extra slack on every side.
 */
export function gateRect({ width, open }) {
  const cap = (0.26 + 0.1) / 2, slack = 0.05, stock = GATE_STOCK;
  const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, open)) * Math.PI / 2;
  const len = gateLeafLength(width);
  const swing = len * Math.sin(a) + stock * Math.cos(a); // deepest leaf point behind the hinge line
  return {
    min: [-width / 2 - cap - slack, -Math.max(cap, swing) - slack],
    max: [width / 2 + cap + slack, Math.max(cap, 0.045 + stock * Math.sin(a)) + slack],
  };
}

// --- one deterministic layout attempt --------------------------------------
function buildOnce(spec, catalog, seed, attempt, grow = 1, camXZ = [VIEWER_DIR[0] * CAM_HINT_DIST, VIEWER_DIR[1] * CAM_HINT_DIST]) {
  const rng = makeRng(seed);
  const plazaR = spec.plaza.radius;
  const ringR = spec.plaza.ring.radius;
  const ringW = spec.plaza.ring.width;
  const ringInner = ringR - ringW / 2;
  const ringOuter = ringR + ringW / 2;
  // Grow the extent / outer placement bands when the layout needs more room.
  const area = spec.area.shape === 'rect'
    ? { shape: 'rect', w: spec.area.w * grow, d: spec.area.d * grow }
    : { shape: 'circle', radius: spec.area.radius * grow };
  const instances = [];

  // Plaza: a darker ground disk, the centre piece (well), and the paved ring.
  instances.push({ id: '__plaza', kind: 'plaza', pos: [0, 0], rotY: 0, scale: 1, radius: plazaR });
  const wellId = plazaCenterId(spec, catalog);
  if (wellId) instances.push({ id: wellId, kind: 'well', pos: [0, 0], rotY: 0, scale: 1 });
  const ringId = 'b-plaza-ring';
  if (catalog.has(ringId)) {
    const segments = Math.max(24, Math.round((TAU * ringR) / 0.8));
    instances.push({ id: ringId, kind: 'ring', pos: [0, 0], rotY: 0, scale: 1, params: { inner: ringInner, outer: ringOuter, segments } });
  }

  // --- buildings: landmarks (best plaza-facing slots) then houses ----------
  const landmarkList = queryCatalog(catalog, spec.landmarks.pick);
  const houseList = queryCatalog(catalog, spec.houses.pick);
  const nLand = landmarkList.length ? rng.int(spec.landmarks.count[0], spec.landmarks.count[1]) : 0;
  const [hLo, hHi] = scaleCount(spec.houses.count, spec.density.houses);
  const nHouse = rng.int(hLo, hHi);
  const arc = spec.houses.arc ?? 1.75;
  const total = nLand + nHouse;

  const landRing = [spec.landmarks.ring[0], spec.landmarks.ring[1] * grow];
  const houseRing = [spec.houses.ring[0], spec.houses.ring[1] * grow];

  const buildings = []; // { entry, pos, rotY, index, kind, lot, dp, strip }
  const lots = [];
  const clearR = Math.max(plazaR, ringOuter); // buildings stay >=1 off this
  const pathWidth = spec.paths.width;
  const pathR = ringR - 0.3; // door paths end just inside the ring centreline
  const fc = spec.landmarks.facing || spec.houses.facing;
  const maxCam = fc.maxCameraAngle / DEG, maxPlaza = fc.maxPlazaAngle / DEG, maxBend = (fc.maxPathBend ?? 8) / DEG;
  const step = 0.5 / DEG;

  // Door yaw for a building at `pos` on a slot `off` radians from the arc
  // centre: start facing the plaza centre, blend toward the camera for lots far
  // from the arc centre. HARD window: the turn s (from plaza-facing toward the
  // camera) must satisfy s >= camMin (door <= maxCameraAngle off the camera,
  // aiming CAM_MARGIN inside it because the final camera is only known after
  // layout), s <= maxPlazaAngle, and the straight door path must still meet the
  // ring within maxPathBend. An empty window returns null (slot rejected).
  function chooseYaw(entry, pos, off) {
    const yP = yawOf([-pos[0], -pos[1]]);
    const yC = yawOf([camXZ[0] - pos[0], camXZ[1] - pos[1]]);
    const diff = wrapAngle(yC - yP);
    const sg = Math.sign(diff) || 1, ad = Math.abs(diff);
    const w = (fc.blend ?? 0.8) * Math.min(1, Math.abs(off) / Math.max(1e-6, arc));
    const camMin = Math.max(0, ad - maxCam);
    const hiS = Math.min(maxPlaza, ad);
    if (camMin > hiS) return null;
    let s = Math.min(Math.max(ad * w, camMin + (camMin > 0 ? CAM_MARGIN : 0)), hiS);
    let dp = doorPath(entry, pos, yP + sg * s, pathR);
    while (s > camMin && (!dp || dp.bend > maxBend)) { s = Math.max(camMin, s - step); dp = doorPath(entry, pos, yP + sg * s, pathR); }
    if (!dp || dp.bend > maxBend) return null;
    return { rotY: wrapAngle(yP + sg * s), dp };
  }

  // Facing-feasible arc: on each side of the arc centre, the largest offset
  // (<= arc) where a representative house just inside the band still has a
  // legal yaw. Slots are spread over that span instead of the full arc, so arc
  // ends that can never meet the caps aren't handed out (deterministic: no rng).
  const probe = houseList[0] || landmarkList[0];
  const probeR = houseRing[0] + (houseRing[1] - houseRing[0]) * 0.3;
  const feasibleSide = (sgn) => {
    if (!probe) return arc;
    let ok = 0;
    for (let off = 0; off <= arc + 1e-9; off += 1 / DEG) {
      const th = AWAY_ANGLE + sgn * off;
      if (!chooseYaw(probe, [probeR * Math.cos(th), probeR * Math.sin(th)], sgn * off)) break;
      ok = off;
    }
    return Math.max(ok, Math.min(arc, 0.35));
  };
  const arcLo = feasibleSide(-1), arcHi = feasibleSide(1);
  // Stratified slots across [centre - arcLo, centre + arcHi]; the centre-most
  // slots (facing the viewer / plaza) go to landmarks, the rest to houses.
  const slots = buildingSlots(total, arcLo, arcHi, AWAY_ANGLE);
  const jitter = ((arcLo + arcHi) / 2 / Math.max(1, total)) * 0.9;

  const paths = [];     // path strip instances, in building order
  const pathObbs = [];  // their OBBs (for lot / fence clearance)
  const rejects = { facing: 0, lots: 0, plaza: 0, area: 0, pathLot: 0, lotPath: 0 }; // placement diagnostics

  function placeBuilding(entry, slotAngle, ring) {
    const tries = spec.retries.placement;
    for (let t = 0; t < tries; t++) {
      // Jitter around the slot (slots already span only the facing-feasible
      // arc, so pulling toward the centre would just crowd the neighbours).
      const theta = slotAngle + rng.float(-1, 1) * jitter;
      const r = rng.float(ring[0], ring[1]);
      const pos = [r * Math.cos(theta), r * Math.sin(theta)];
      const yaw = chooseYaw(entry, pos, wrapAngle(theta - AWAY_ANGLE));
      if (!yaw) { rejects.facing++; continue; } // facing window empty here: hard reject
      const { rotY, dp } = yaw;
      const place = { x: pos[0], z: pos[1], rotY };
      const lot = obbFromRect(entry.lot || entry.footprint, place);
      if (lots.some((l) => obbOverlap(lot, l, 1.0))) { rejects.lots++; continue; } // lots apart
      const fp = obbFromRect(entry.footprint, place);
      if (obbPointDistance(fp, [0, 0]) < clearR + 1.0) { rejects.plaza++; continue; } // off plaza/ring
      if (obbCorners(obbFromRect(rectXZ(entry.bounds), place)).some((c) => !pointInArea(area, c, 0.5))) { rejects.area++; continue; }
      // The door path may not cut through another lot, and this lot may not
      // sit on an earlier building's path.
      const strip = pathStrip(dp, pathWidth);
      if (lots.some((l) => obbOverlap(strip.obb, l, 0.2))) { rejects.pathLot++; continue; }
      if (pathObbs.some((o) => obbOverlap(lot, o, 0.2))) { rejects.lotPath++; continue; }
      return { entry, pos, rotY, lot, dp, strip };
    }
    return null;
  }

  function addBuilding(placed, kind) {
    placed.index = buildings.length; placed.kind = kind;
    buildings.push(placed); lots.push(placed.lot);
    const inst = { id: placed.entry.id, kind, pos: placed.pos, rotY: placed.rotY, scale: 1, lot: placed.index };
    placed.inst = inst;
    instances.push(inst);
    // One strip from the door snap to the plaza RING (ends on the centreline).
    const s = placed.strip;
    if (s.length >= 0.5) {
      const p = { id: spec.paths.id, kind: 'path', pos: s.mid, rotY: s.rotY, scale: 1, lot: placed.index, params: { length: s.length, width: pathWidth } };
      paths.push(p); pathObbs.push(s.obb);
    }
  }

  // Landmarks first on the centre slots.
  for (let i = 0; i < nLand; i++) {
    const entry = pickEntry(rng, landmarkList, spec.landmarks.prefer);
    const placed = placeBuilding(entry, slots[i], landRing);
    if (placed) addBuilding(placed, 'landmark');
  }
  // Houses fill the remaining (outer) slots.
  for (let i = nLand; i < total; i++) {
    const entry = pickEntry(rng, houseList, spec.houses.prefer);
    const placed = placeBuilding(entry, slots[i], houseRing);
    if (placed) addBuilding(placed, 'house');
  }
  const houses = buildings.filter((b) => b.kind === 'house');
  instances.push(...paths);

  // --- fences: ONE continuous run per fenced house around back/left/right +
  //     a gated front (gap = pathWidth + 0.6 centred on the door path) --------
  const fenceEntry = catalog.get(spec.fences.id);
  // Gate ids were validated in compileTown. Without gates there are no fenced
  // lots: the fence-continuity check needs both front runs covered, and a
  // covered front with no gate would wall the door path in.
  const gateId = spec.fences.gate ? spec.fences.gate.id : null;
  if (fenceEntry && gateId) {
    const fp = fenceEntry.footprint;
    const segLen = Math.max(0.8, (fp.max[0] - fp.min[0])); // one piece's world length (local X span)
    const gate = pathWidth + 0.6;
    for (const h of houses) {
      if (!rng.chance(spec.fences.probability)) continue;
      const lot = h.entry.lot; if (!lot) continue;
      const place = { x: h.pos[0], z: h.pos[1], rotY: h.rotY };
      // Where the door-path centreline crosses the front fence line (local x;
      // ~0 since the path leaves the door within maxPathBend). The gate gap
      // is centred there.
      const snapL = h.entry.pathSnaps?.[0]?.pos || [0, 0, lot.max[1]];
      const ld = rotDir(h.dp.dir, -h.rotY); // path direction in house-local coords
      const gx = snapL[0] + (ld[1] > 1e-3 ? ld[0] * (lot.max[1] - snapL[2]) / ld[1] : 0);
      // Perimeter runs in LOCAL coords, each listed START -> END. The front is
      // split around the gate and the two front runs START at the gate edge,
      // so a (uniform-scale) piece never intrudes into the gate; the last
      // piece of every run is clamped to the end (overlapping, never gapping).
      const runs = [
        ['back', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]]],
        ['left', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['right', [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [gx - gate / 2, lot.max[1]], [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [gx + gate / 2, lot.max[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
      ];
      const segs = [];
      for (const [edge, la, lb] of runs) {
        const c1 = toWorldXZ(la, place);
        const c2 = toWorldXZ(lb, place);
        const len = dist(c1, c2);
        if (len < 0.6) continue; // sub-run shorter than a gate half: skip
        const ed = norm([c2[0] - c1[0], c2[1] - c1[1]]);
        const rotY = Math.atan2(-ed[1], ed[0]); // local X runs along the edge
        const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(len / segLen)); // overlap, never gap
        for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
          // Chain from the start; clamp the last piece to end at the run's end.
          const tc = (k === n - 1) ? Math.max(segLen / 2, len - segLen / 2) : segLen * (k + 0.5);
          segs.push({ id: spec.fences.id, kind: 'fence', pos: [c1[0] + ed[0] * tc, c1[1] + ed[1] * tc], rotY, scale: 1, lot: h.index, edge, seg: segLen });
        }
      }
      // A segment on any path (e.g. a neighbour's door path) would leave a
      // hole in the run: leave this lot unfenced instead of gapping it.
      const onPath = segs.some((s) => {
        const o = obbFromRect(fenceEntry.footprint, { x: s.pos[0], z: s.pos[1], rotY: s.rotY });
        return pathObbs.some((po) => obbOverlap(o, po, 0.05));
      });
      if (onPath || !segs.length) continue;
      instances.push(...segs);
      // The gate fills the front gap: hinge-line centre on the fence line at
      // the door-path crossing, local X along the edge (= house yaw).
      {
        const gp = toWorldXZ([gx, lot.max[1]], place);
        // Leaves swing inward: cap `open` so a leaf tip stays >= 0.15 in front
        // of the house footprint (lot front -> footprint front = the setback).
        const setback = lot.max[1] - h.entry.footprint.max[1] - 0.15;
        const openMax = setback <= GATE_STOCK ? 0 : gateOpenMax(gate, setback);
        instances.push({
          id: gateId, kind: 'gate', pos: gp, rotY: h.rotY, scale: 1, lot: h.index, edge: 'front',
          width: +gate.toFixed(3), open: +Math.min(spec.fences.gate.open, openMax).toFixed(3),
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // --- scatter: trees, bushes, then props ----------------------------------
  const scattered = []; // {pos, radius}
  const fpRadius = (entry, scale = 1) => {
    const f = entry.footprint;
    return Math.max(f.max[0] - f.min[0], f.max[1] - f.min[1]) / 2 * scale;
  };
  const boundsRadius = (entry, scale = 1) => {
    const b = entry.bounds;
    let r = 0;
    for (const x of [b.min[0], b.max[0]]) for (const z of [b.min[2], b.max[2]]) r = Math.max(r, Math.hypot(x, z));
    return r * scale;
  };
  const offPaths = (pos, rad) => paths.every((p) => {
    const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
    return segPointDistance(A, B, pos) > pathWidth / 2 + rad + 0.4;
  });
  const insideLot = (pos, grow2 = 0) => lots.some((l) => obbPointDistance(l, pos) <= grow2);

  function sampleArea() {
    if (area.shape === 'rect') return [rng.float(-area.w / 2, area.w / 2), rng.float(-area.d / 2, area.d / 2)];
    const r = Math.sqrt(rng.next()) * area.radius;
    const a = rng.next() * TAU;
    return [r * Math.cos(a), r * Math.sin(a)];
  }

  // View corridor: the wedge between the camera and the core — keep tall trees
  // out of it so they never hide a façade.
  let vfx = 0, vfz = 0;
  for (const b of buildings) { const f = rotDir([0, 1], b.rotY); vfx += f[0]; vfz += f[1]; }
  const viewDir = (vfx || vfz) ? norm([vfx, vfz]) : VIEWER_DIR;
  const rMax = houseRing[1];
  const inViewCorridor = (pos) => {
    const along = pos[0] * viewDir[0] + pos[1] * viewDir[1];
    const lateral = Math.abs(pos[0] * viewDir[1] - pos[1] * viewDir[0]);
    return along > 0 && lateral < rMax + 2;
  };

  function scatter(cfg, kind, mult, { jitter: jit = false } = {}) {
    const list = queryCatalog(catalog, cfg.pick);
    if (!list.length) return;
    const [lo, hi] = scaleCount(cfg.count, mult);
    const n = rng.int(lo, hi);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      for (let t = 0; t < spec.retries.placement; t++) {
        const entry = rng.pick(list);
        const scale = jit ? rng.float(0.9, 1.15) : 1;
        const pos = sampleArea();
        const rad = fpRadius(entry, scale);
        if (!pointInArea(area, pos, boundsRadius(entry, scale) + 0.3)) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) < clearR + rad + 0.5) continue; // off plaza + ring
        if (cfg.maxRadius && Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) > cfg.maxRadius) continue; // keep dressing near the town
        if (insideLot(pos, rad)) continue;
        if (kind === 'tree') {
          if (inViewCorridor(pos)) continue;
          if (Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) < houseRing[0]) continue; // keep the green open
          if (!offPaths(pos, rad + 2.0)) continue;
        }
        if (!offPaths(pos, rad)) continue;
        if (scattered.some((s) => Math.hypot(s.pos[0] - pos[0], s.pos[1] - pos[1]) < s.radius + rad + 0.6)) continue;
        scattered.push({ pos, radius: rad });
        instances.push({ id: entry.id, kind, pos, rotY: rng.float(0, TAU), scale });
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  scatter(spec.trees, 'tree', spec.density.trees, { jitter: true });
  scatter(spec.bushes, 'bush', spec.density.bushes, { jitter: true });

  // Props: near a house (inside/edge of its lot), off footprint and paths.
  if (houses.length) {
    const list = queryCatalog(catalog, spec.props.pick);
    if (list.length) {
      const [lo, hi] = scaleCount(spec.props.count, spec.density.props);
      const n = rng.int(lo, hi);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        for (let t = 0; t < spec.retries.placement; t++) {
          const entry = rng.pick(list);
          const h = rng.pick(houses);
          const rad = fpRadius(entry);
          const lx = rng.sign() * rng.float(1.6, Math.max(1.7, (h.entry.footprint.max[0]) + 1.2));
          const lz = rng.float(h.entry.footprint.min[1] + 0.3, h.entry.footprint.max[1] - 0.3);
          const pos = toWorldXZ([lx, lz], { x: h.pos[0], z: h.pos[1], rotY: h.rotY });
          const fpO = obbFromRect(h.entry.footprint, { x: h.pos[0], z: h.pos[1], rotY: h.rotY });
          if (!pointInArea(area, pos, rad + 0.3)) continue;
          if (obbPointDistance(fpO, pos) < rad + 0.2) continue;
          if (Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) < clearR + rad) continue;
          if (!offPaths(pos, rad)) continue;
          if (scattered.some((s) => Math.hypot(s.pos[0] - pos[0], s.pos[1] - pos[1]) < s.radius + rad + 0.3)) continue;
          scattered.push({ pos, radius: rad });
          instances.push({ id: entry.id, kind: 'prop', pos, rotY: rng.float(0, TAU), scale: 1, lot: h.index });
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const camera = computeCamera(instances, buildings, catalog);
  // Facing is recorded against the camera actually suggested for this layout.
  const camGround = [camera.pos[0], camera.pos[2]];
  for (const b of buildings) b.inst.facing = facingAngles(b.pos, b.rotY, camGround);
  return { name: spec.name, seed, attempt, grow: +grow.toFixed(3), area, camera, instances, camHint: camXZ.map((v) => +v.toFixed(2)),
    facingArc: [+(arcLo * DEG).toFixed(1), +(arcHi * DEG).toFixed(1)], buildingsWanted: total, placementRejects: rejects };
}

// Rank offsets before adding the absolute angle. Symmetric slots have the
// same priority; explicitly prefer the lower-angle slot on ties. Subtracting
// AWAY_ANGLE from absolute angles made ulp noise reverse their order in Chrome.
export function buildingSlots(total, arcLo, arcHi, center) {
  return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, index) => {
    const offset = -arcLo + ((index + 0.5) / total) * (arcLo + arcHi);
    return { angle: center + offset, rank: Math.round(Math.abs(offset) * 1e12), index };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank || a.index - b.index).map((s) => s.angle);
}

const worstCameraAngle = (town) => town.instances.reduce((m, i) => Math.max(m, i.facing ? i.facing.cameraAngle : 0), 0);

// --- camera suggestion from the final layout -------------------------------
const norm3 = (v) => { const l = Math.hypot(v[0], v[1], v[2]) || 1; return [v[0] / l, v[1] / l, v[2] / l]; };
function computeCamera(instances, buildings, catalog) {
  // Mean outward direction the doors look (= where the camera should stand).
  let fx = 0, fz = 0;
  for (const b of buildings) { const f = rotDir([0, 1], b.rotY); fx += f[0]; fz += f[1]; }
  const dir = (fx || fz) ? norm([fx, fz]) : VIEWER_DIR;

  // Core sample points (ground + top) over every CORE instance.
  const vfov = CAMERA_FOV * Math.PI / 180;
  let el = CAMERA_ELEV_DEG * Math.PI / 180; // searched up to CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG below
  const targetY = 2.5;
  const core = [];
  for (const inst of instances) {
    if (!CORE_KINDS.has(inst.kind) || (inst.id !== '__plaza' && !catalog.get(inst.id))) continue;
    const rect = rectForInst(inst, catalog);
    const e = catalog.get(inst.id);
    const yTop = (inst.kind === 'ring') ? 0.1 : ((e?.bounds.max[1] || 0) * (inst.scale || 1));
    for (const c of obbCorners(obbFromRect(rect, { x: inst.pos[0], z: inst.pos[1], rotY: inst.rotY }, inst.scale || 1))) {
      core.push([c[0], 0, c[1]], [c[0], yTop, c[1]]);
    }
  }
  // Target = centroid of the core XZ bounds.
  const center = [0, 0];
  if (core.length) {
    let a = Infinity, b = -Infinity, c = Infinity, d = -Infinity;
    for (const p of core) { a = Math.min(a, p[0]); b = Math.max(b, p[0]); c = Math.min(c, p[2]); d = Math.max(d, p[2]); }
    center[0] = (a + b) / 2; center[1] = (c + d) / 2;
  }

  // Fit against an EFFECTIVE viewport: the left PANEL_LEFT_CUT of the frame is
  // reserved for the panel, so require the core to sit in screen x in
  // [PANEL_LEFT_CUT, 1] (normalised nx in [leftLim, 1]).
  const tanV = Math.tan(vfov / 2), tanH = tanV * 16 / 9;
  const leftLim = -(1 - 2 * PANEL_LEFT_CUT);
  // Project the core from a camera at slant distance D looking at `tgt`.
  // Returns normalised screen extents (nx, ny in [-1, 1] = frame edge).
  const extents = (D, tgt) => {
    const eye = [tgt[0] + dir[0] * D * Math.cos(el), targetY + D * Math.sin(el), tgt[1] + dir[1] * D * Math.cos(el)];
    const fwd = norm3([tgt[0] - eye[0], targetY - eye[1], tgt[1] - eye[2]]);
    const right = norm3([-fwd[2], 0, fwd[0]]);
    const up = [right[1] * fwd[2] - right[2] * fwd[1], right[2] * fwd[0] - right[0] * fwd[2], right[0] * fwd[1] - right[1] * fwd[0]];
    let x0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y0 = Infinity, y1 = -Infinity, behind = false;
    for (const p of core) {
      const v = [p[0] - eye[0], p[1] - eye[1], p[2] - eye[2]];
      const zc = v[0] * fwd[0] + v[1] * fwd[1] + v[2] * fwd[2];
      if (zc <= 0) { behind = true; break; }
      const nx = (v[0] * right[0] + v[1] * right[1] + v[2] * right[2]) / zc / tanH;
      const ny = (v[0] * up[0] + v[1] * up[1] + v[2] * up[2]) / zc / tanV;
      x0 = Math.min(x0, nx); x1 = Math.max(x1, nx); y0 = Math.min(y0, ny); y1 = Math.max(y1, ny);
    }
    return { behind, x0, x1, y0, y1, right };
  };
  // For each D, slide the target so the core is centred in the effective
  // viewport (x in [leftLim, 1], y in [-1, 1]), then bisect for the smallest
  // D at which the core spans <= CAMERA_FILL of that viewport on both axes
  // (the binding axis then fills ~CAMERA_FILL, a small margin either side).
  const wantX = (leftLim + 1) / 2;
  const groundFwd = [-dir[0], -dir[1]];
  const fitAt = (D) => {
    let tgt = [...center];
    let ex = extents(D, tgt);
    for (let k = 0; k < 6 && !ex.behind; k++) {
      const sx = ((ex.x0 + ex.x1) / 2 - wantX) * tanH * D;
      const sy = ((ex.y0 + ex.y1) / 2) * tanV * D / Math.sin(el);
      tgt = [tgt[0] + ex.right[0] * sx + groundFwd[0] * sy, tgt[1] + ex.right[2] * sx + groundFwd[1] * sy];
      ex = extents(D, tgt);
    }
    const fx = ex.behind ? Infinity : (ex.x1 - ex.x0) / (1 - leftLim);
    const fy = ex.behind ? Infinity : (ex.y1 - ex.y0) / 2;
    const inside = !ex.behind && ex.x0 >= leftLim && ex.x1 <= 1 && ex.y0 >= -1 && ex.y1 <= 1;
    return { tgt, fx, fy, ok: inside && Math.max(fx, fy) <= CAMERA_FILL };
  };
  const solve = () => {
    let lo = 8, hi = 220, best = fitAt(hi);
    for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) {
      const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
      const r = fitAt(mid);
      if (r.ok) { hi = mid; best = r; } else lo = mid;
    }
    return { D: hi, ...best };
  };
  // A wide arc makes the fit width-limited, leaving empty ground below the
  // town. Raising the elevation (up to CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG) spreads the core
  // vertically: pick the lowest elevation whose minor-axis fill is within
  // 0.01 of the best (the binding axis is CAMERA_FILL at every elevation).
  const cands = [];
  for (let deg = CAMERA_ELEV_DEG; deg <= CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG + 1e-9; deg += 1) {
    el = deg * Math.PI / 180;
    cands.push({ deg, el, ...solve() });
  }
  const bestMinor = Math.max(...cands.map((c) => Math.min(c.fx, c.fy)));
  const pick = cands.find((c) => Math.min(c.fx, c.fy) >= bestMinor - 0.01);
  el = pick.el;
  const D = +pick.D.toFixed(2);
  const tgt = pick.tgt;
  center[0] = tgt[0]; center[1] = tgt[1];
  const horiz = D * Math.cos(el), vert = D * Math.sin(el);
  // A rough radius for callers that want it.
  let radius = 1;
  for (const p of core) radius = Math.max(radius, Math.hypot(p[0] - center[0], p[2] - center[1]));
  return {
    pos: [center[0] + dir[0] * horiz, targetY + vert, center[1] + dir[1] * horiz],
    target: [center[0], targetY, center[1]],
    dir, distance: D, fov: CAMERA_FOV, elevation: pick.deg, radius, center, leftCut: PANEL_LEFT_CUT, fill: CAMERA_FILL,
    fillX: +pick.fx.toFixed(3), fillY: +pick.fy.toFixed(3),
  };
}

// --- town-level retry loop -------------------------------------------------
// The facing caps are HARD. checks.js (owned separately) may grow its own
// facing check; until it does, the compiler appends 'facing-caps' so a town
// whose recorded facing breaks houses.facing is retried, not accepted.
function addFacingCheck(town, spec) {
  if (town.report.checks.some((c) => /facing/.test(c.name))) return;
  const { maxCameraAngle, maxPlazaAngle } = spec.houses.facing;
  const failures = [];
  for (const i of town.instances) {
    if (!i.facing) continue;
    if (i.facing.cameraAngle > maxCameraAngle || i.facing.plazaAngle > maxPlazaAngle) {
      failures.push({ msg: `${i.id} faces ${i.facing.cameraAngle} deg off camera / ${i.facing.plazaAngle} off plaza`, a: i.pos });
    }
  }
  town.report.checks.push({ name: 'facing-caps', ok: failures.length === 0, failures, warn: false });
  town.report.ok = town.report.ok && failures.length === 0;
}

const failScore = (report) => report.checks.reduce((n, c) => n + (c.warn ? 0 : (c.failures?.length || 0)), 0);

export function compileTown(rawSpec, catalog, { seed } = {}) {
  const spec = applyDefaults(rawSpec);
  const gateErr = gateSpecError(spec, catalog);
  if (gateErr) throw new Error(`spec: ${gateErr}`);
  const baseSeed = (seed ?? spec.seed ?? 1) >>> 0;
  const townRetries = spec.retries.town;
  let best = null;
  let grow = 1;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= townRetries; attempt++) {
    const subSeed = attempt === 0 ? baseSeed : deriveSeed(baseSeed, attempt);
    // Door yaws are chosen against a camera guess; the real camera comes from
    // the finished layout. If the recorded camera angles overshoot the cap,
    // redo the layout (same sub-seed) against that camera, keeping the best.
    const cap = spec.houses.facing.maxCameraAngle;
    let town = buildOnce(spec, catalog, subSeed, attempt, grow);
    for (let k = 0; k < 3 && worstCameraAngle(town) > cap; k++) {
      const again = buildOnce(spec, catalog, subSeed, attempt, grow, [town.camera.pos[0], town.camera.pos[2]]);
      if (worstCameraAngle(again) <= worstCameraAngle(town)) town = again;
      else break;
    }
    town.seed = baseSeed;       // the requested seed (subSeed is the attempt's)
    town.subSeed = subSeed;
    town.report = validate(town, catalog, spec);
    addFacingCheck(town, spec);
    town.ok = town.report.ok;
    if (town.ok) return town;
    if (!best || failScore(town.report) < failScore(best.report)) best = town;
    // Auto-grow the extent when buildings can't all fit (house-count fail).
    const hc = town.report.checks.find((c) => c.name === 'house-count-in-range');
    if (hc && !hc.ok) grow = Math.min(1.7, grow * 1.12);
  }
  best.ok = false;
  return best;
}

exec
/bin/bash -lc 'cat src/compiler/checks.js; cat src/compiler/spec.js; cat tools/compile.mjs' in /workspace/cel-town
 succeeded in 0ms:
// Town validation — pure 2D math over a compiled town (no three.js). Returns
// { ok, checks:[{ name, ok, failures:[{msg,a,b}], warn? }], stats }. `ok` is
// true when every non-warn check passes. Rotated-OBB SAT is used throughout so
// placement is judged on the real rotated footprints, not axis-aligned boxes.
import { applyDefaults } from './spec.js';
import { math as Math } from './math.js';
import { CORE_KINDS, rectForInst } from './compile.js';
import {
  obbFromRect, rectXZ, obbCorners, obbOverlap, obbSeparation, obbPointDistance,
  segPointDistance, strutEnds, toWorldXZ, pointInArea, norm, dist, rotDir,
} from './geometry.js';

const place = (inst) => ({ x: inst.pos[0], z: inst.pos[1], rotY: inst.rotY });

function footprintObb(inst, catalog) {
  const e = catalog.get(inst.id);
  return obbFromRect(e.footprint, place(inst), inst.scale || 1);
}
function boundsObb(inst, catalog) {
  return obbFromRect(rectForInst(inst, catalog), place(inst), inst.scale || 1);
}
/** World [x,z] -> local [x,z] under a placement (inverse of toWorldXZ). */
function localOf([wx, wz], pl) {
  const dx = wx - pl.x, dz = wz - pl.z, c = Math.cos(pl.rotY || 0), s = Math.sin(pl.rotY || 0);
  return [dx * c - dz * s, dx * s + dz * c];
}

export function validate(town, catalog, spec) {
  const checks = [];
  const add = (name, failures, warn = false) => checks.push({ name, ok: failures.length === 0, failures, warn });
  const inst = town.instances;
  const area = town.area;
  const plazaR = (inst.find((i) => i.kind === 'plaza')?.radius) ?? spec.plaza.radius;
  const ringR = spec.plaza.ring.radius, ringW = spec.plaza.ring.width;
  const ringInner = ringR - ringW / 2, ringOuter = ringR + ringW / 2;
  const clearR = Math.max(plazaR, ringOuter); // keep-clear radius for buildings

  const buildings = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'house' || i.kind === 'landmark');
  const houses = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'house');
  const paths = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'path');
  const fences = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'fence');
  const scatter = inst.filter((i) => ['tree', 'bush', 'prop'].includes(i.kind));
  const solids = inst.filter((i) => ['house', 'landmark', 'prop', 'tree', 'bush', 'well'].includes(i.kind));

  // 1. footprint-overlap — no two solid footprints may overlap.
  {
    const f = [];
    const obbs = solids.map((i) => footprintObb(i, catalog));
    for (let a = 0; a < solids.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < solids.length; b++) {
      if (obbOverlap(obbs[a], obbs[b])) f.push({ msg: `${solids[a].id} overlaps ${solids[b].id}`, a: solids[a].pos, b: solids[b].pos });
    }
    add('footprint-overlap', f);
  }

  // 2. roof-overlap — building roof boxes (XZ) may not intersect.
  {
    const f = [];
    const rhs = buildings.map((h) => { const e = catalog.get(h.id); return e.roof ? obbFromRect(rectXZ(e.roof), place(h), h.scale || 1) : null; });
    for (let a = 0; a < buildings.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < buildings.length; b++) {
      if (rhs[a] && rhs[b] && obbOverlap(rhs[a], rhs[b])) f.push({ msg: `roof ${buildings[a].id} overlaps ${buildings[b].id}`, a: buildings[a].pos, b: buildings[b].pos });
    }
    add('roof-overlap', f);
  }

  // 3. setback — lots apart (gap >= 0) and footprints >= 1 off the ring/plaza.
  {
    const f = [];
    const lots = buildings.map((h) => obbFromRect(catalog.get(h.id).lot || catalog.get(h.id).footprint, place(h), h.scale || 1));
    for (let a = 0; a < buildings.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < buildings.length; b++) {
      if (obbSeparation(lots[a], lots[b]) < -0.01) f.push({ msg: `lots overlap ${buildings[a].id}/${buildings[b].id}`, a: buildings[a].pos, b: buildings[b].pos });
    }
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const d = obbPointDistance(footprintObb(h, catalog), [0, 0]) - clearR;
      if (d < 1 - 0.01) f.push({ msg: `${h.id} only ${d.toFixed(2)} from plaza/ring`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('setback', f);
  }

  // 4. trees-off-paths — trees/bushes clear of every path centreline.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const s of scatter.filter((i) => i.kind !== 'prop')) {
      const e = catalog.get(s.id);
      const rad = Math.max(e.footprint.max[0] - e.footprint.min[0], e.footprint.max[1] - e.footprint.min[1]) / 2 * (s.scale || 1);
      for (const p of paths) {
        const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
        if (segPointDistance(A, B, s.pos) < p.params.width / 2 + rad - 0.01) { f.push({ msg: `${s.id} on path`, a: s.pos }); break; }
      }
    }
    add('trees-off-paths', f);
  }

  // 5. trees-outside-lots — trees/bushes never inside a building lot.
  {
    const f = [];
    const lots = buildings.map((h) => obbFromRect(catalog.get(h.id).lot || catalog.get(h.id).footprint, place(h), h.scale || 1));
    for (const s of scatter.filter((i) => i.kind !== 'prop')) {
      if (lots.some((l) => obbPointDistance(l, s.pos) <= 0)) f.push({ msg: `${s.id} inside a lot`, a: s.pos });
    }
    add('trees-outside-lots', f);
  }

  // 6. door-reachable — each building's path snap meets a path end, and that
  //    path's other end lands in the plaza RING band (ring connectivity then
  //    links every door to the plaza).
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const snap = e.pathSnaps?.[0];
      if (!snap) { f.push({ msg: `${h.id} has no path snap`, a: h.pos }); continue; }
      const sp = toWorldXZ([snap.pos[0], snap.pos[2]], place(h));
      let reached = false;
      for (const p of paths.filter((p) => p.lot === h.lot)) {
        const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
        const dA = dist(A, sp), dB = dist(B, sp);
        const plazaEnd = dA < dB ? B : A;
        const nearHouse = Math.min(dA, dB);
        const rEnd = Math.hypot(plazaEnd[0], plazaEnd[1]);
        if (nearHouse <= 0.6 && rEnd >= ringInner - 0.4 && rEnd <= ringOuter + 0.4) { reached = true; break; }
      }
      if (!reached) f.push({ msg: `${h.id} door path does not reach the plaza ring`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('door-reachable', f);
  }

  // 7. within-area — every instance's rotated bounds corners inside the area.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const i of inst) {
      if (i.kind === 'plaza') { if (i.radius > (area.shape === 'rect' ? Math.min(area.w, area.d) / 2 : area.radius)) f.push({ msg: 'plaza exceeds area', a: [0, 0] }); continue; }
      if (!catalog.get(i.id)) continue;
      if (obbCorners(boundsObb(i, catalog)).some((c) => !pointInArea(area, c, 0))) f.push({ msg: `${i.id} pokes outside the area`, a: i.pos });
    }
    add('within-area', f);
  }

  // 8. fences-not-on-paths — fence segments clear of every path.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const s of fences) {
      const o = footprintObb(s, catalog);
      if (paths.some((p) => obbOverlap(o, obbFromRect(rectForInst(p, catalog), place(p))))) f.push({ msg: `fence on a path`, a: s.pos });
    }
    add('fences-not-on-paths', f);
  }

  // 9/10. fence continuity + gate alignment. A fenced lot's perimeter (minus
  //       the front gate) must have no gap > 0.5, and the front gate must sit
  //       on the door path.
  {
    const contFails = [];
    const gateFails = [];
    const pathWidth = spec.paths.width;
    const gate = pathWidth + 0.6;
    const byLot = new Map();
    for (const s of fences) { if (!byLot.has(s.lot)) byLot.set(s.lot, []); byLot.get(s.lot).push(s); }
    for (const [lotIdx, segs] of byLot) {
      const h = houses.find((x) => x.lot === lotIdx);
      if (!h) continue;
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const lot = e.lot || e.footprint;
      const pl = place(h);
      const seg0 = segs[0].seg || 3.0;
      // Perimeter runs (local coords). Front split around the centred gate.
      const runs = [
        ['back', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]]],
        ['left', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['right', [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]], [-gate / 2, lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [gate / 2, lot.max[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
      ];
      for (const [edge, la, lb] of runs) {
        const c1 = toWorldXZ(la, pl), c2 = toWorldXZ(lb, pl);
        const L = dist(c1, c2);
        if (L < 0.6) continue; // sub-run intentionally omitted
        const dir = norm([c2[0] - c1[0], c2[1] - c1[1]]);
        // Covered intervals from segments whose centre lies near this run.
        const iv = [];
        for (const s of segs) {
          const t = (s.pos[0] - c1[0]) * dir[0] + (s.pos[1] - c1[1]) * dir[1];
          const nd = Math.abs((s.pos[0] - c1[0]) * dir[1] - (s.pos[1] - c1[1]) * dir[0]);
          if (nd > 0.6 || t < -seg0 || t > L + seg0) continue; // not on this run
          iv.push([t - seg0 / 2, t + seg0 / 2]);
        }
        const gap = maxGap(iv, L, 0.5); // ignore 0.5 tolerance at the run ends
        if (gap > 0.5) contFails.push({ msg: `${h.id} fence gap ${gap.toFixed(2)} on ${edge}`, a: h.pos });
      }
      // Gate: no front fence may cover the door-path crossing (local x ~ 0).
      const frontSegs = segs.filter((s) => Math.abs(localOf(s.pos, pl)[1] - lot.max[1]) < 0.6);
      const blocks = frontSegs.some((s) => {
        const lx = localOf(s.pos, pl)[0];
        return Math.abs(lx) - seg0 / 2 < pathWidth / 2 - 0.05; // seg overlaps the gate corridor
      });
      if (blocks) gateFails.push({ msg: `${h.id} fence blocks its front gate`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('fence-continuity', contFails);
    add('fence-gate-aligned', gateFails);
  }

  // gate-at-door — verify explicit gates and the actual opening on the fence line.
  {
    const f = [];
    const gates = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'gate');
    const fenced = buildings.filter((h) => h.lot != null && fences.some((s) => s.lot === h.lot && s.edge === 'front'));
    for (const g of gates) {
      if (!fenced.some((h) => h.lot === g.lot)) f.push({ msg: `orphan gate instance ${inst.indexOf(g)} lot ${g.lot}`, a: g.pos });
    }
    for (const h of fenced) {
      const label = `instance ${inst.indexOf(h)} ${h.id} lot ${h.lot}`;
      const gs = gates.filter((g) => g.lot === h.lot);
      if (gs.length !== 1 || gs[0]?.edge !== 'front') {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} needs exactly one front gate (found ${gs.length})`, a: h.pos });
        continue;
      }
      const g = gs[0], pl = place(h);
      const e = catalog.get(h.id), lot = e.lot || e.footprint;
      const frontZ = lot.max[1] * (h.scale || 1);
      const [gx, gz] = localOf(g.pos, pl);
      if (!(Math.abs(gz - frontZ) <= 0.35)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate off front fence line`, a: g.pos });
      const lotPaths = paths.filter((p) => p.lot === h.lot);
      const p = lotPaths[0];
      if (lotPaths.length !== 1) f.push({ msg: `${label} needs one door path (found ${lotPaths.length})`, a: g.pos });
      if (p) {
        const d = rotDir([0, 1], p.rotY);
        const lateral = Math.abs((g.pos[0] - p.pos[0]) * d[1] - (g.pos[1] - p.pos[1]) * d[0]);
        if (!(lateral <= 0.25)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate ${lateral.toFixed(2)} off door path centreline`, a: g.pos });
        const width = (p.params?.width ?? p.width ?? spec.paths.width) * (p.scale || 1);
        if (!(g.width * (g.scale || 1) >= width)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate narrower than door path (${g.width} < ${width})`, a: g.pos });
      }
      // A zero-depth OBB tests the opening interval on the exact front line.
      // Use fence footprints, including rotation and scale; touching endpoints
      // are allowed, but any positive intersection closes part of the gap.
      if (Number.isFinite(g.width) && g.width > 0) {
        const half = g.width * (g.scale || 1) / 2;
        const opening = obbFromRect({ min: [gx - half, frontZ], max: [gx + half, frontZ] }, pl);
        if (fences.some((s) => obbOverlap(opening, footprintObb(s, catalog), -1e-6))) {
          f.push({ msg: `${label} gate opening overlaps a fence segment`, a: g.pos });
        }
      } else f.push({ msg: `${label} gate has invalid width`, a: g.pos });
    }
    add('gate-at-door', f);
  }

  // house-facing — derive direction independently of the recorded diagnostics.
  {
    const f = [];
    const limits = applyDefaults(spec).houses.facing;
    const angle = (a, b) => Math.atan2(Math.abs(a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0]), a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1]) * 180 / Math.PI;
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const localDir = e.door?.facing ?? e.pathSnaps?.[0]?.dir;
      const label = `instance ${inst.indexOf(h)} ${h.id}`;
      if (!localDir || !town.camera?.pos || !Number.isFinite(h.rotY)) {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} cannot compute facing: missing door direction, rotation or camera (cameraAngle=NaN plazaAngle=NaN)`, a: h.pos });
        continue;
      }
      const dir = rotDir([localDir[0], localDir[2]], h.rotY);
      const cameraAngle = angle(dir, [town.camera.pos[0] - h.pos[0], town.camera.pos[2] - h.pos[1]]);
      const plazaAngle = angle(dir, [-h.pos[0], -h.pos[1]]);
      const angles = `cameraAngle=${cameraAngle.toFixed(2)} plazaAngle=${plazaAngle.toFixed(2)}`;
      if (!(cameraAngle <= limits.maxCameraAngle && plazaAngle <= limits.maxPlazaAngle)) {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} ${angles} exceeds limits ${limits.maxCameraAngle}/${limits.maxPlazaAngle}`, a: h.pos });
      }
      if (h.facing && ['cameraAngle', 'plazaAngle'].some((key) => !Number.isFinite(h.facing[key]) || Math.abs(h.facing[key] - ({ cameraAngle, plazaAngle })[key]) > 2)) {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} facing data drift: ${angles}, recorded ${h.facing.cameraAngle}/${h.facing.plazaAngle}`, a: h.pos });
      }
    }
    add('house-facing', f);
  }

  // 11. house-count-in-range (density-scaled range).
  {
    const [lo, hi] = scaledRange(spec.houses.count, spec.density.houses);
    const f = (houses.length >= lo && houses.length <= hi) ? [] : [{ msg: `house count ${houses.length} outside [${lo},${hi}]`, a: [0, 0] }];
    add('house-count-in-range', f);
  }

  // 12. framing (warn).
  {
    const f = framingFailures(town, catalog);
    add('framing', f, true);
  }

  const ok = checks.every((c) => c.warn || c.ok);
  const stats = {
    houses: houses.length, landmarks: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'landmark').length,
    paths: paths.length, fences: fences.length,
    trees: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'tree').length,
    bushes: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'bush').length,
    props: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'prop').length,
    instances: inst.length,
  };
  return { ok, checks, stats };
}

function scaledRange([lo, hi], mult = 1) {
  const m = mult == null ? 1 : mult;
  const slo = Math.max(0, Math.round(lo * m));
  return [slo, Math.max(slo, Math.round(hi * m))];
}

/** Largest uncovered gap inside [tol, L-tol] given covered intervals. */
function maxGap(intervals, L, tol = 0) {
  if (L <= 0) return 0;
  const iv = intervals.map(([a, b]) => [Math.max(0, a), Math.min(L, b)]).filter(([a, b]) => b > a).sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0]);
  let cursor = tol, worst = 0;
  for (const [a, b] of iv) {
    if (a > cursor) worst = Math.max(worst, a - cursor);
    cursor = Math.max(cursor, b);
  }
  if (L - tol > cursor) worst = Math.max(worst, L - tol - cursor);
  return worst;
}

// Pure-math frustum check honouring the panel-reserved left region.
function framingFailures(town, catalog) {
  const cam = town.camera;
  if (!cam) return [];
  const eye = cam.pos, tgt = cam.target;
  const fwd = norm3([tgt[0] - eye[0], tgt[1] - eye[1], tgt[2] - eye[2]]);
  const right = norm3(cross(fwd, [0, 1, 0]));
  const up = cross(right, fwd);
  const halfV = (cam.fov || 42) * Math.PI / 360;
  const halfH = Math.atan(Math.tan(halfV) * 16 / 9);
  const tanH = Math.tan(halfH), tanV = Math.tan(halfV);
  const leftLim = -(1 - 2 * (cam.leftCut ?? 0)) - 0.02;
  const f = [];
  for (const i of town.instances) {
    if (!CORE_KINDS.has(i.kind) || (i.id !== '__plaza' && !catalog.get(i.id))) continue;
    const e = catalog.get(i.id);
    const rect = rectForInst(i, catalog);
    const yTop = (i.kind === 'ring') ? 0.1 : (e?.bounds?.max?.[1] ?? 0);
    for (const c of obbCorners(obbFromRect(rect, { x: i.pos[0], z: i.pos[1], rotY: i.rotY }, i.scale || 1))) {
      for (const y of [0, yTop]) {
        const v = [c[0] - eye[0], y - eye[1], c[1] - eye[2]];
        const zc = dot3(v, fwd); if (zc <= 0) { f.push({ msg: `${i.id} behind camera`, a: i.pos }); break; }
        const nx = dot3(v, right) / zc / tanH, ny = dot3(v, up) / zc / tanV;
        if (nx < leftLim || nx > 1.02 || Math.abs(ny) > 1.02) { f.push({ msg: `${i.id} outside frame`, a: i.pos }); break; }
      }
    }
  }
  return f;
}

const dot3 = (a, b) => a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2];
const cross = (a, b) => [a[1] * b[2] - a[2] * b[1], a[2] * b[0] - a[0] * b[2], a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0]];
function norm3(v) { const l = Math.hypot(v[0], v[1], v[2]) || 1; return [v[0] / l, v[1] / l, v[2] / l]; }
// Town spec format (compiler v3). A spec is plain JSON (see specs/hamlet.json)
// describing WHAT a town contains; the seeded compiler decides WHERE. Count
// ranges are inclusive [min, max] drawn by the seeded RNG. `pick` is a tag
// filter resolved against the catalog.
//
// Spec shape (all keys optional unless noted — DEFAULTS fill the rest):
//   name    string label.
//   seed    default seed when none is passed to compileTown.
//   area    { shape:'circle', radius } | { shape:'rect', w, d }  — town extent,
//           centred on the origin.
//   plaza   { center, radius, ring }. center = catalog id (e.g. 'cv-well') or a
//           tag query { category:'well', ... }; it is placed at the origin.
//           radius = the plaza disk / keep-clear radius. ring describes the
//           paved loop every door path must join (see below).
//   plaza.ring { style:'builder'|'cobble-tiles', width, radius }. A flat paved
//           loop centred on the well at `radius` (centreline), `width` wide.
//           'builder' = a single toon ring mesh (b-plaza-ring); 'cobble-tiles'
//           falls back to the builder ring for now. Every door path ends ON this
//           ring (overlapping ~0.3), and ring connectivity counts as reaching
//           the plaza, so no door path dead-ends at open ground.
//   landmarks { count:[min,max], pick, ring:[r0,r1], placement:'plaza-facing' }.
//           Civic buildings (category:'landmark'). They are placed BEFORE houses
//           and take the best plaza-facing slots (centre of the far arc), door
//           facing the plaza, with a path to the ring. ring = their radius band.
//   houses  { count:[min,max], pick, prefer, ring:[rMin,rMax], arc }.
//           pick/prefer are tag filters; ring = radius band around the plaza;
//           arc = half-angle (radians) of the placement arc on the side AWAY
//           from the camera (fronts then face the viewer).
//   houses.facing { maxCameraAngle, maxPlazaAngle, blend, maxPathBend } (v3,
//           also used by landmarks). Each building's door yaw starts facing the
//           plaza centre and is blended toward the camera by
//           blend * (|slot offset| / arc), i.e. more at the arc ends, then
//           clamped so (horizontal, degrees) the door is <= maxCameraAngle off
//           the direction to the camera and <= maxPlazaAngle off the direction
//           to the plaza centre, and its straight door path still lands on the
//           ring at most maxPathBend degrees off the door direction. Every
//           house/landmark instance records facing:{cameraAngle, plazaAngle}
//           (degrees, horizontal, from the building origin) measured against
//           the town's suggested camera (town.camera.pos) and the origin.
//   trees   { count:[min,max], pick }        scattered, off lots/paths/plaza.
//   bushes  { count:[min,max], pick, maxRadius }  maxRadius (trees too) keeps
//           dressing within that distance of the plaza centre (optional).
//   props   { count:[min,max], pick, near }  near:'house' lets props sit inside
//           a lot by the house (never on footprint/path).
//   paths   { style:'builder', id, width }   one strip per building to the ring.
//   fences  { id, probability, edges }       per fenced house: ONE continuous
//           run around back+left+right+front with a GATE GAP on the front edge
//           centred on the door path. edges is accepted for back-compat but the
//           v2 fence always runs the full perimeter (minus the gate).
//   fences.gate { id, open } (v3). One gate per fenced lot, in the front gap:
//           { id, kind:'gate', pos, rotY, scale:1, lot, edge:'front', width,
//           open }. pos = hinge-line centre: where the door-path centreline
//           crosses the front fence line. rotY = the house yaw (gate local X
//           runs along the front edge, +Z points out of the lot). width = the
//           gap width. open = 0..1 of a 90 deg inward swing (default 0.55,
//           ~50 deg; the compiler lowers it per lot so leaves clear the house).
//           gate:null = no gates AND no fenced lots (a fenced front needs its
//           gate). A gate id missing from the catalog or not category 'gate'
//           is an error (validateSpec with a catalog, and compileTown).
//   density { houses, trees, bushes, props } multipliers (default 1) scaling the
//           count ranges (rounded, clamped to >= 0). If many houses don't fit,
//           the compiler auto-grows area.radius / houses.ring over its town
//           retries before giving up.
//   camera  { mode:'auto' }                  auto = suggest from final layout.
//   retries { placement, town }              placement = per-item rejection
//           retries; town = recompiles with a derived sub-seed on validate fail.
//
// pick/prefer tag filter fields: { category, style (string the entry's style[]
// must contain), source ('mesh'|'builder') }.

export const DEFAULTS = {
  name: 'Hamlet',
  seed: 1,
  area: { shape: 'circle', radius: 34 },
  plaza: { center: 'cv-well', radius: 6, ring: { style: 'builder', width: 1.8, radius: 6 } },
  landmarks: { count: [1, 2], pick: { category: 'landmark' }, ring: [13, 19], placement: 'plaza-facing' },
  houses: { count: [4, 6], pick: { category: 'house' }, prefer: { source: 'mesh' }, ring: [13, 22], arc: 1.75,
    facing: { maxCameraAngle: 45, maxPlazaAngle: 50, blend: 0.8, maxPathBend: 18 } },
  trees: { count: [10, 16], pick: { category: 'tree' } },
  bushes: { count: [6, 12], pick: { category: 'bush' }, maxRadius: 28 },
  props: { count: [2, 5], pick: { category: 'prop' }, near: 'house' },
  paths: { style: 'builder', id: 'b-path-6', width: 2.3 },
  fences: { id: 'cv-picket-fence', probability: 0.6, edges: ['back', 'left', 'right', 'front'], gate: { id: 'b-gate', open: 0.55 } },
  density: { houses: 1, trees: 1, bushes: 1, props: 1 },
  camera: { mode: 'auto' },
  retries: { placement: 40, town: 12 },
};

const isRange = (v) => Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 2 && v[0] <= v[1];

/** Merge a (possibly partial) spec over DEFAULTS. Shallow-per-section. */
export function applyDefaults(spec = {}) {
  const out = { ...DEFAULTS, ...spec };
  for (const k of ['area', 'plaza', 'landmarks', 'houses', 'trees', 'bushes', 'props', 'paths', 'fences', 'density', 'camera', 'retries']) {
    out[k] = { ...DEFAULTS[k], ...(spec[k] || {}) };
  }
  // plaza.ring is nested one level deeper; deep-merge it too.
  out.plaza.ring = { ...DEFAULTS.plaza.ring, ...((spec.plaza && spec.plaza.ring) || {}) };
  // v3 nested sections.
  out.houses.facing = { ...DEFAULTS.houses.facing, ...((spec.houses && spec.houses.facing) || {}) };
  out.fences.gate = (spec.fences && spec.fences.gate === null) ? null
    : { ...DEFAULTS.fences.gate, ...((spec.fences && spec.fences.gate) || {}) };
  return out;
}

/** Apply a density multiplier to an inclusive [min,max] count range. */
export function scaleCount([lo, hi], mult = 1) {
  const m = mult == null ? 1 : mult;
  const slo = Math.max(0, Math.round(lo * m));
  const shi = Math.max(slo, Math.round(hi * m));
  return [slo, shi];
}

/**
 * Gate-config problem for a defaulted spec against a resolved catalog Map, or
 * null if fine. gate:null is allowed (no gates, and then no fenced lots: a
 * front edge without its gate would break the gate/fence invariants).
 */
export function gateSpecError(spec, catalog) {
  const g = spec.fences.gate;
  if (g == null) return null;
  if (typeof g.id !== 'string' || !g.id) return 'fences.gate.id must be a catalog id (or set fences.gate to null)';
  if (!catalog) return null;
  const e = catalog.get(g.id);
  if (!e) return `fences.gate.id '${g.id}' is not in the catalog`;
  if (e.tags?.category !== 'gate') return `fences.gate.id '${g.id}' is category '${e.tags?.category}', not 'gate'`;
  return null;
}

/** Throw on a malformed spec. Returns the defaulted spec on success. Pass the
 *  resolved catalog to also validate catalog references (fences.gate.id). */
export function validateSpec(rawSpec, catalog) {
  const spec = applyDefaults(rawSpec);
  const err = (m) => { throw new Error(`spec: ${m}`); };
  const a = spec.area;
  if (a.shape === 'circle') { if (!(a.radius > 0)) err('circle area needs radius > 0'); }
  else if (a.shape === 'rect') { if (!(a.w > 0 && a.d > 0)) err('rect area needs w,d > 0'); }
  else err(`unknown area.shape ${a.shape}`);
  if (!(spec.plaza.radius > 0)) err('plaza.radius must be > 0');
  if (!(spec.plaza.ring.radius > 0 && spec.plaza.ring.width > 0)) err('plaza.ring needs radius,width > 0');
  for (const k of ['houses', 'trees', 'bushes', 'props', 'landmarks']) {
    if (!isRange(spec[k].count)) err(`${k}.count must be an inclusive [min,max]`);
  }
  if (!isRange(spec.houses.ring) || spec.houses.ring[0] <= 0) err('houses.ring must be [rMin,rMax], rMin > 0');
  if (!isRange(spec.landmarks.ring) || spec.landmarks.ring[0] <= 0) err('landmarks.ring must be [r0,r1], r0 > 0');
  for (const k of ['houses', 'trees', 'bushes', 'props']) {
    if (!(spec.density[k] >= 0)) err(`density.${k} must be >= 0`);
  }
  if (!(spec.paths.width > 0)) err('paths.width must be > 0');
  const fc = spec.houses.facing;
  if (!(fc.maxCameraAngle > 0 && fc.maxCameraAngle <= 180)) err('houses.facing.maxCameraAngle must be in (0,180]');
  if (!(fc.maxPlazaAngle >= 0 && fc.maxPlazaAngle <= 180)) err('houses.facing.maxPlazaAngle must be in [0,180]');
  if (spec.fences.gate && !(spec.fences.gate.open >= 0 && spec.fences.gate.open <= 1)) err('fences.gate.open must be in [0,1]');
  const ge = gateSpecError(spec, catalog);
  if (ge) err(ge);
  return spec;
}
// Node CLI for the town compiler. Pure JS path (no three.js): bounds.json is
// read with fs and resolved via the same resolve-all.js the browser uses.
//   npm run compile -- --seed 7 [--spec specs/hamlet.json] [--out out/]
//   npm run compile -- --sweep 50     (seeds 1..50: pass rate + top failures)
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolveAll } from '../src/catalog/resolve-all.js';
import { compileTown } from '../src/compiler/compile.js';
import { validateSpec } from '../src/compiler/spec.js';

function arg(name, def) {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
  return i >= 0 ? (process.argv[i + 1] ?? true) : def;
}
const root = new URL('../', import.meta.url);
const read = (p) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL(p, root), 'utf8'));

const bounds = read('src/catalog/bounds.json');
const catalog = resolveAll(bounds);
const specPath = arg('spec', 'specs/hamlet.json');
const spec = validateSpec(read(specPath), catalog);
// --town-retries N overrides spec.retries.town (0 = show first-attempt failures).
if (arg('town-retries', null) != null) spec.retries.town = Number(arg('town-retries'));
const outDir = arg('out', 'out/');

function failing(report) {
  return report.checks.filter((c) => !c.warn && !c.ok).map((c) => c.name);
}
function summarize(town) {
  const r = town.report;
  return `seed ${String(town.seed).padStart(6)} attempt ${town.attempt} ${town.ok ? 'OK ' : 'FAIL'} `
    + `· ${r.stats.houses}h ${r.stats.paths}p ${r.stats.fences}f ${r.stats.trees}t ${r.stats.bushes}b ${r.stats.props}pr`
    + (town.ok ? '' : ` · fails: ${failing(r).join(',')}`);
}

const sweep = arg('sweep', null);
const stats = arg('stats', null); // --stats N: facing / camera / gate stats over seeds 1..N
mkdirSync(new URL(outDir, root), { recursive: true });

if (stats) {
  const N = Number(stats);
  let wc = 0, wp = 0, gates = 0, fenced = 0, dsum = 0, gateOff = 0, openMin = Infinity, openMax = 0, openSum = 0;
  for (let seed = 1; seed <= N; seed++) {
    const town = compileTown(spec, catalog, { seed });
    for (const b of town.instances) {
      if (!b.facing) continue;
      wc = Math.max(wc, b.facing.cameraAngle); wp = Math.max(wp, b.facing.plazaAngle);
    }
    const g = town.instances.filter((i) => i.kind === 'gate');
    gates += g.length;
    // Gate hinge-line centre vs its lot's door-path centreline (perpendicular distance).
    for (const gi of g) {
      openMin = Math.min(openMin, gi.open); openMax = Math.max(openMax, gi.open); openSum += gi.open;
      const p =town.instances.find((i) => i.kind === 'path' && i.lot === gi.lot);
      if (!p) { gateOff = Infinity; continue; }
      const d = [Math.sin(p.rotY), Math.cos(p.rotY)];
      gateOff = Math.max(gateOff, Math.abs((gi.pos[0] - p.pos[0]) * d[1] - (gi.pos[1] - p.pos[1]) * d[0]));
    }
    fenced += new Set(town.instances.filter((i) => i.kind === 'fence').map((i) => i.lot)).size;
    dsum += town.camera.distance;
    if (N <= 10) {
      const c = town.camera;
      console.log(`seed ${seed} ${town.ok ? 'OK' : 'FAIL'} att ${town.attempt} cam D ${c.distance.toFixed(1)} el ${c.elevation} fill ${c.fillX}/${c.fillY} pos ${c.pos.map((v) => v.toFixed(1))} `
        + `arc ${town.facingArc} want ${town.buildingsWanted} got ${town.instances.filter((i) => i.facing).length} rej ${JSON.stringify(town.placementRejects)} `
        + `facing ${town.instances.filter((i) => i.facing).map((i) => `${i.facing.cameraAngle.toFixed(0)}/${i.facing.plazaAngle.toFixed(0)}`).join(' ')} gates ${g.length}`);
    }
  }
  console.log(`stats ${N} seeds · worst cameraAngle ${wc.toFixed(1)} · worst plazaAngle ${wp.toFixed(1)} · gates ${gates} / fenced lots ${fenced} (max gate offset from path centreline ${gateOff.toFixed(3)}; open min/mean/max ${gates ? `${openMin}/${(openSum / gates).toFixed(3)}/${openMax}` : '-'}) · mean cam distance ${(dsum / N).toFixed(1)}`);
} else if (sweep) {
  const N = Number(sweep);
  let firstPass = 0, retriedPass = 0;
  const failCounts = {};
  const rows = [];
  for (let seed = 1; seed <= N; seed++) {
    const town = compileTown(spec, catalog, { seed });
    if (town.attempt === 0 && town.ok) firstPass++;
    if (town.ok) retriedPass++;
    else for (const name of failing(town.report)) failCounts[name] = (failCounts[name] || 0) + 1;
    rows.push({ seed, ok: town.ok, attempt: town.attempt, fails: town.ok ? [] : failing(town.report), stats: town.report.stats });
  }
  const topFails = Object.entries(failCounts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  const result = {
    spec: specPath, seeds: N,
    firstAttemptPass: firstPass, firstAttemptRate: +(firstPass / N).toFixed(3),
    afterRetryPass: retriedPass, afterRetryRate: +(retriedPass / N).toFixed(3),
    topFailingChecks: topFails.map(([name, count]) => ({ name, count })),
    rows,
  };
  writeFileSync(new URL(`${outDir}sweep.json`, root), JSON.stringify(result, null, 1) + '\n');
  console.log(`sweep ${N} seeds · first-attempt ${firstPass}/${N} (${(100 * firstPass / N).toFixed(0)}%) · after-retry ${retriedPass}/${N} (${(100 * retriedPass / N).toFixed(0)}%)`);
  console.log('top failing checks:', topFails.length ? topFails.map(([n, c]) => `${n}×${c}`).join(' ') : 'none');
  console.log(`wrote ${outDir}sweep.json`);
} else {
  const seed = Number(arg('seed', spec.seed ?? 1));
  const town = compileTown(spec, catalog, { seed });
  const file = `${outDir}town-${seed}.json`;
  writeFileSync(new URL(file, root), JSON.stringify({ ...town }, null, 1) + '\n');
  console.log(summarize(town));
  console.log(`wrote ${file}`);
}

// Turn a compiled town into three.js objects. Browser-only (imports the loader
// and builders). buildTown(town, scene) adds the world (sky/hills/ground),
// the plaza disk and every instance, resolving async mesh loads before the
// returned Promise settles.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { place } from '../catalog/loader.js';
import { PALETTE, toonMaterial } from '../toon.js';
import { makeGround, makeHills, makeSky } from '../builders.js';

function plazaDisk(radius) {
  const disk = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(radius, 48), toonMaterial(PALETTE.grassDark));
  disk.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  disk.position.y = 0.02; // just above makeGround's green patch
  disk.receiveShadow = true;
  return disk;
}

export function buildTown(town, scene) {
  scene.add(makeSky());
  scene.add(makeHills());
  scene.add(makeGround()); // disk radius 70 covers the default area (r<=34)

  const loads = [];
  for (const inst of town.instances) {
    if (inst.kind === 'plaza') { scene.add(plazaDisk(inst.radius)); continue; }
    const [x, z] = inst.pos;
    // Gates carry their gap width / swing as top-level instance fields.
    const params = inst.kind === 'gate'
      ? { ...(inst.params || {}), ...(inst.width != null ? { width: inst.width } : {}), ...(inst.open != null ? { open: inst.open } : {}) }
      : inst.params;
    loads.push(
      place(inst.id, { x, z, rotY: inst.rotY, params, scale: inst.scale || 1 })
        .then((obj) => { obj.userData.inst = inst; scene.add(obj); return obj; }),
    );
  }
  return Promise.all(loads);
}
// Compile a town in the browser and render it with the toon stage using the
// suggested camera. Query params:
//   ?seed=N     seed (default: the spec's seed)
//   ?spec=path  spec to compile (default: the bundled specs/hamlet.json)
//   ?debug=1    turn the debug anchors on at load (checkbox reflects it)
//   ?cam=/?target=  honoured by stage.js, overriding the suggested camera
// Sets window.__ready once rendered (tools/shots.mjs waits on it).
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { createStage, townCoreBoxes } from './stage.js';
import { RESOLVED } from './catalog/loader.js';
import { compileTown, facingAngles } from './compiler/compile.js';
import { validateSpec } from './compiler/spec.js';
import { buildTown } from './compiler/runtime.js';
import { anchorGizmo, setLayers } from './catalog/debug.js';
import { createPost } from './post/post.js';
import { parseOptions } from './post/options.js';
import { createLookPanel } from './post/ui.js';
import { maybeMeasurePerf } from './post/perf.js';
import HAMLET from '../specs/hamlet.json';

const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
const seed = q.has('seed') ? Number(q.get('seed')) : (HAMLET.seed ?? 1);
const specPath = q.get('spec');

// Single source of truth: the bundled hamlet spec, unless ?spec points elsewhere
// (fetched from the served tree).
async function loadSpec() {
  if (!specPath || specPath.endsWith('specs/hamlet.json')) return HAMLET;
  try { return await (await fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}${specPath}`)).json(); }
  catch { return HAMLET; }
}

(async () => {
  const rawSpec = await loadSpec();
  const spec = validateSpec(rawSpec, RESOLVED);
  const town = compileTown(spec, RESOLVED, { seed });
  const cam = town.camera;

  const stage = createStage({
    camPos: cam.pos, target: cam.target, minDistance: 8, maxDistance: 220, shadowSize: 48, fog: [120, 320],
    fitRadius: cam.radius + 4,
  });
  const { renderer, scene } = stage;

  // Debug anchors: an anchorGizmo + lot OBB outline per instance. On at load
  // when ?debug=1 (the checkbox reflects the initial state).
  const debugOn = q.get('debug') === '1' || q.get('debug') === 'true';
  const gizmos = new THREE.Group();
  gizmos.visible = debugOn;
  scene.add(gizmos);

  await buildTown(town, scene);
  // Frame the built core tightly right of the panel (~20% of the width), in
  // both camera modes (the persp fit keeps the suggested view direction).
  const coreBoxes = townCoreBoxes(scene);
  stage.setIsoFit(coreBoxes, { panelFrac: 0.2 });
  stage.setPerspectiveFit(coreBoxes, { panelFrac: 0.2, fill: 0.86 });

  // Build gizmos after instances exist, reading each placed pivot's transform.
  scene.traverse((o) => {
    const inst = o.userData.inst;
    if (!inst || !o.userData.entry) return;
    const g = anchorGizmo(o.userData.entry);
    g.position.copy(o.position);
    g.rotation.copy(o.rotation);
    g.scale.copy(o.scale);
    gizmos.add(g);
    if ((inst.kind === 'house' || inst.kind === 'landmark') && o.userData.entry.lot) gizmos.add(lotOutline(o.userData.entry, inst));
  });
  setLayers(gizmos, { footprint: true, lot: true, bounds: false, roof: true, door: true, snaps: true });

  // --- panel ---------------------------------------------------------------
  const r = town.report;
  byId('spec').textContent = (town.name || 'spec');
  byId('seed').textContent = String(town.seed);
  byId('attempt').textContent = `${town.attempt}${town.ok ? '' : ' (best)'}`;
  const verdict = byId('verdict');
  verdict.textContent = town.ok ? 'OK' : 'FAIL';
  verdict.style.color = town.ok ? '#2e7d32' : '#c0392b';
  const checks = byId('checks');
  for (const c of r.checks) {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'chk ' + (c.warn && !c.ok ? 'warn' : c.ok ? 'ok' : 'bad');
    row.innerHTML = `<span>${c.name}${c.warn ? ' *' : ''}</span><span>${c.ok ? '✓' : c.failures.length}</span>`;
    checks.appendChild(row);
  }
  // The compiler certifies facing against town.camera; the page renders with
  // the runtime perspective fit (or a ?cam/?target override), whose eye can
  // differ. Re-measure against the camera actually rendered and flag overruns
  // honestly (warn only; the compiler's own report is left untouched).
  if (stage.camera.isPerspectiveCamera) {
    const cp = stage.camera.position;
    let worst = null;
    for (const i of town.instances) {
      if (!i.facing) continue;
      const a = facingAngles(i.pos, i.rotY, [cp.x, cp.z]).cameraAngle;
      if (!worst || a > worst.angle) worst = { angle: a, id: i.id };
    }
    const cap = spec.houses.facing.maxCameraAngle;
    window.__renderedFacing = worst ? { worstCameraAngle: worst.angle, id: worst.id, cap, ok: worst.angle <= cap } : null;
    if (worst && worst.angle > cap) {
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'chk warn';
      row.title = `${worst.id}: ${worst.angle.toFixed(1)} deg off the rendered camera (cap ${cap})`;
      row.innerHTML = `<span>facing (rendered camera) *</span><span>${worst.angle.toFixed(1)}°</span>`;
      checks.appendChild(row);
    }
  }
  const stat = document.createElement('div');
  stat.className = 'muted';
  stat.style.marginTop = '4px';
  stat.textContent = `${r.stats.houses}h ${r.stats.paths}p ${r.stats.fences}f ${r.stats.trees}t ${r.stats.bushes}b ${r.stats.props}pr`;
  checks.appendChild(stat);

  byId('new').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const next = Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6);
    q.set('seed', next);
    location.search = q.toString();
  });
  const anchors = byId('anchors');
  anchors.checked = debugOn;
  anchors.addEventListener('change', (e) => { gizmos.visible = e.target.checked; });

  // --- post chain + Look panel --------------------------------------------
  const post = createPost(renderer, scene, stage.camera, parseOptions());
  post.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
  post.setHullVisible(post.options.hull);
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => post.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight));
  createLookPanel({ post, stage });

  let ready = false;
  (function loop() {
    stage.controls.update();
    post.render(stage.camera);
    if (!ready) { ready = true; window.__ready = true; maybeMeasurePerf(renderer, post, () => stage.camera); }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  })();
})();

function lotOutline(entry, inst) {
  const pts = [[entry.lot.min[0], entry.lot.min[1]], [entry.lot.max[0], entry.lot.min[1]], [entry.lot.max[0], entry.lot.max[1]], [entry.lot.min[0], entry.lot.max[1]]]
    .map(([x, z]) => new THREE.Vector3(x, 0.08, z));
  const line = new THREE.LineLoop(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts), new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x3ddc84, depthTest: false, transparent: true }));
  line.renderOrder = 997;
  line.position.set(inst.pos[0], 0, inst.pos[1]);
  line.rotation.y = inst.rotY;
  line.userData.layer = 'lot';
  return line;
}
