// Runtime loader for catalog entries: imported GLBs (Draco) and code builders
// come back as the same thing — a pivot Group at the entry's local origin,
// already scaled into world units, front facing +Z, toon-shaded with outlines.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { GLTFLoader } from 'three/addons/loaders/GLTFLoader.js';
import { DRACOLoader } from 'three/addons/loaders/DRACOLoader.js';
import { mergeVertices } from 'three/addons/utils/BufferGeometryUtils.js';
import { toonMaterial, toonVertexColorMaterial, outlineMaterial } from '../toon.js';
import { FOLIAGE_CATEGORIES } from '../post/options.js';
import { BUILDERS } from './builders-registry.js';
import { resolveAll } from './resolve-all.js';
import BOUNDS from './bounds.json';

const BASE = import.meta.env.BASE_URL;

// Resolved (world-unit) catalog — same Map the compiler consumes, built here
// from the Vite-imported bounds.json (see resolve-all.js, shared with the CLI).
export const RESOLVED = resolveAll(BOUNDS);
export const getEntry = (id) => {
  const e = RESOLVED.get(id);
  if (!e) throw new Error(`[catalog] unknown entry ${id}`);
  return e;
};

// Draco decoder is vendored in public/draco/ (copied from three/examples) so
// it works offline and without a CDN.
const draco = new DRACOLoader().setDecoderPath(`${BASE}draco/`);
const gltfLoader = new GLTFLoader().setDRACOLoader(draco);
const _gltf = new Map();
function loadGLTF(path, category) {
  if (!_gltf.has(path)) _gltf.set(path, gltfLoader.loadAsync(BASE + path).then((g) => toonify(g.scene, category)));
  return _gltf.get(path);
}

// Smooth a crown's normals by position only, so tree/bush foliage shades as
// rounded three-tone bands instead of faceted low-poly. Colours stay per
// vertex (Cozy crowns vary colour per facet, so a position+colour weld never
// merged anything and the crowns stayed faceted).
function weldFoliage(geo) {
  const g = geo.index ? geo.toNonIndexed() : geo.clone();
  const pos = g.attributes.position;
  const P = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) { P[i * 3] = pos.getX(i); P[i * 3 + 1] = pos.getY(i); P[i * 3 + 2] = pos.getZ(i); }
  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3));
  const key = (i) => `${Math.round(P[i * 3] * 1e3)},${Math.round(P[i * 3 + 1] * 1e3)},${Math.round(P[i * 3 + 2] * 1e3)}`;
  const acc = new Map();
  const a = new THREE.Vector3(), b = new THREE.Vector3(), c = new THREE.Vector3();
  for (let t = 0; t < pos.count; t += 3) {
    a.fromArray(P, t * 3); b.fromArray(P, t * 3 + 3); c.fromArray(P, t * 3 + 6);
    const n = c.sub(b).cross(a.sub(b)); // area-weighted face normal
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const id = key(t + k);
      const v = acc.get(id) || acc.set(id, new THREE.Vector3()).get(id);
      v.add(n);
    }
  }
  const N = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
    const v = acc.get(key(i)).clone().normalize();
    N[i * 3] = v.x; N[i * 3 + 1] = v.y; N[i * 3 + 2] = v.z;
  }
  g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3));
  // Cozy crowns also bake a different shade into each facet, which fights the
  // toon bands. Flatten the leaf colours (green-dominant vertices) to their
  // mean so the three-tone ramp alone shapes the crown; trunks keep theirs.
  const col = g.attributes.color;
  if (col) {
    const C = new Float32Array(col.count * col.itemSize);
    const leaf = [];
    const m = [0, 0, 0];
    for (let i = 0; i < col.count; i++) {
      const r = col.getX(i), gg = col.getY(i), b = col.getZ(i);
      for (let k = 0; k < col.itemSize; k++) C[i * col.itemSize + k] = col.getComponent(i, k);
      if (gg > r * 1.05 && gg > b) { leaf.push(i); m[0] += r; m[1] += gg; m[2] += b; }
    }
    if (leaf.length) {
      for (const i of leaf) { C[i * col.itemSize] = m[0] / leaf.length; C[i * col.itemSize + 1] = m[1] / leaf.length; C[i * col.itemSize + 2] = m[2] / leaf.length; }
    }
    g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, col.itemSize));
  }
  return g;
}

// Outline hull geometry: imported meshes are flat-shaded (split normals), so a
// hull pushed along those normals cracks open at every hard edge. Weld by
// position and recompute smooth normals for the hull only.
const _hull = new WeakMap();
function hullGeometry(geo) {
  if (_hull.has(geo)) return _hull.get(geo);
  const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  // Copy positions into a plain attribute (glTF/Draco may hand us interleaved).
  const src = geo.attributes.position;
  const arr = new Float32Array(src.count * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < src.count; i++) { arr[i * 3] = src.getX(i); arr[i * 3 + 1] = src.getY(i); arr[i * 3 + 2] = src.getZ(i); }
  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr, 3));
  if (geo.index) g.setIndex(geo.index);
  const welded = mergeVertices(g, 1e-3);
  welded.computeVertexNormals();
  _hull.set(geo, welded);
  return welded;
}

const OUTLINE_WORLD = 0.035; // matches the builders' typical ink weight

function toonify(root, category) {
  const foliage = FOLIAGE_CATEGORIES.has(category);
  const ramp = foliage ? 'foliage' : 'default';
  const meshes = [];
  root.traverse((o) => { if (o.isMesh) meshes.push(o); });
  for (const m of meshes) {
    const hasVC = !!m.geometry.attributes.color;
    if (foliage) m.geometry = weldFoliage(m.geometry); // smooth crown normals
    m.material = hasVC ? toonVertexColorMaterial({ ramp }) : toonMaterial(m.material.color ?? 0xffffff, { ramp });
    m.castShadow = true;
    m.receiveShadow = true;
    if (foliage) m.userData.foliage = true; // post prepass: no crease ink in crowns
    const edge =new THREE.Mesh(hullGeometry(m.geometry), null);
    edge.userData.outline = true;
    edge.castShadow = false;
    m.add(edge);
  }
  return root;
}

/**
 * Build one instance of a catalog entry. Always async (meshes load).
 * `params` overrides a builder entry's params (e.g. a path's length); `scale`
 * applies an extra uniform multiplier on top of the entry's world scale.
 */
export async function instantiate(id, { params, scale = 1 } = {}) {
  const e = getEntry(id);
  const pivot = new THREE.Group();
  pivot.name = id;
  pivot.userData.entry = e;
  let content;
  if (e.source === 'mesh') {
    content = (await loadGLTF(e.path, e.tags.category)).clone(true);
    content.scale.setScalar(e.scale);
    content.position.y = (e.yOffset || 0) * e.scale;
    if (e.yawOffset) content.rotation.y = e.yawOffset; // align door to +Z (dormant: all 0)
    // Outline thickness is applied in object space, so undo the kit scale.
    const mat = outlineMaterial(OUTLINE_WORLD / e.scale);
    content.traverse((o) => { if (o.userData.outline) o.material = mat; });
  } else {
    content = BUILDERS[e.builder]({ ...(e.params || {}), ...(params || {}) });
    if (e.scale !== 1) content.scale.setScalar(e.scale);
    if (e.yawOffset) content.rotation.y = e.yawOffset; // dormant: all 0
  }
  pivot.add(content);
  if (scale !== 1) pivot.scale.setScalar(scale);
  return pivot;
}

/** Instantiate and place at {x, z, rotY}, with optional params/scale override. */
export async function place(id, { x = 0, z = 0, rotY = 0, params, scale = 1 } = {}) {
  const obj = await instantiate(id, { params, scale });
  obj.position.set(x, 0, z);
  obj.rotation.y = rotY;
  return obj;
}
// Door finder for single-mesh Cozy Village buildings. For each GLB prints the
// bbox and, for each cardinal wall, the ground-band protrusion of a LOCALIZED
// step (a door threshold is narrow, not the full wall). Run:
//   node tools/inspect-door.mjs a.glb b.glb
import { NodeIO } from '@gltf-transform/core';
import { ALL_EXTENSIONS } from '@gltf-transform/extensions';
import draco3d from 'draco3dgltf';
import * as THREE from 'three';

const io = new NodeIO().registerExtensions(ALL_EXTENSIONS)
  .registerDependencies({ 'draco3d.decoder': await draco3d.createDecoderModule() });
const r3 = (n) => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;

for (const f of process.argv.slice(2)) {
  const doc = await io.read(f);
  const pts = [];
  for (const node of doc.getRoot().listNodes()) {
    const mesh = node.getMesh(); if (!mesh) continue;
    const M = new THREE.Matrix4().fromArray(node.getWorldMatrix());
    for (const prim of mesh.listPrimitives()) {
      const pos = prim.getAttribute('POSITION');
      const v = [0, 0, 0];
      for (let i = 0; i < pos.getCount(); i++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(...pos.getElement(i, v)).applyMatrix4(M));
    }
  }
  const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(pts);
  const size = new THREE.Vector3(); box.getSize(size);
  // Body footprint from the mid-height band (ignores roof/step overhangs).
  const midBand = pts.filter((p) => p.y > box.min.y + size.y * 0.3 && p.y < box.min.y + size.y * 0.6);
  const mb = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(midBand);
  // Low band for step detection.
  const low = pts.filter((p) => p.y < box.min.y + 0.3);
  // For each side, measure the max protrusion of the low band past the body
  // wall, counting only points actually past the wall (a localized step).
  const probe = (axis, sign, wall) => {
    const pts2 = low.filter((p) => sign * (p[axis] - wall) > 0.02);
    if (!pts2.length) return { protrude: 0, span: 0, at: 0 };
    const other = axis === 'z' ? 'x' : 'z';
    const os = pts2.map((p) => p[other]);
    const protr = Math.max(...pts2.map((p) => sign * (p[axis] - wall)));
    return { protrude: r3(protr), span: r3(Math.max(...os) - Math.min(...os)), at: r3(sign > 0 ? Math.max(...pts2.map(p=>p[axis])) : Math.min(...pts2.map(p=>p[axis]))) };
  };
  const sides = {
    '+z': probe('z', +1, mb.max.z), '-z': probe('z', -1, mb.min.z),
    '+x': probe('x', +1, mb.max.x), '-x': probe('x', -1, mb.min.x),
  };
  console.log(f.split('/').pop());
  console.log('  bbox min', box.min.toArray().map(r3), 'max', box.max.toArray().map(r3), 'size', size.toArray().map(r3));
  console.log('  body(mid) x', [r3(mb.min.x), r3(mb.max.x)], 'z', [r3(mb.min.z), r3(mb.max.z)]);
  for (const [k, s] of Object.entries(sides)) console.log(`  ${k}: protrude=${s.protrude} span=${s.span} at=${s.at}`);
}
// Computes bounds for every catalog entry from real geometry and writes
// src/catalog/bounds.json (derived numbers only — no licensed geometry).
//   meshes:   glTF-Transform + Draco decoder over the GLB in public/
//   builders: construct the builder under three.js in node, walk its meshes
// All numbers are SOURCE units, before scale / yOffset. Run: npm run catalog:bounds
import { NodeIO } from '@gltf-transform/core';
import { ALL_EXTENSIONS } from '@gltf-transform/extensions';
import draco3d from 'draco3dgltf';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { CATALOG } from '../src/catalog/catalog.js';
import { BUILDERS } from '../src/catalog/builders-registry.js';
import { validateEntry } from '../src/catalog/schema.js';

const io = new NodeIO().registerExtensions(ALL_EXTENSIONS)
  .registerDependencies({ 'draco3d.decoder': await draco3d.createDecoderModule() });

async function meshPoints(e) {
  const file = new URL('../public/' + e.path, import.meta.url).pathname;
  if (!existsSync(file)) return null;
  const doc = await io.read(file);
  const pts = []; let tris = 0;
  for (const node of doc.getRoot().listNodes()) {
    const mesh = node.getMesh(); if (!mesh) continue;
    const M = new THREE.Matrix4().fromArray(node.getWorldMatrix());
    for (const prim of mesh.listPrimitives()) {
      const pos = prim.getAttribute('POSITION'); const idx = prim.getIndices();
      tris += (idx ? idx.getCount() : pos.getCount()) / 3;
      const v = [0, 0, 0];
      for (let i = 0; i < pos.getCount(); i++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(...pos.getElement(i, v)).applyMatrix4(M));
    }
  }
  return { pts, tris };
}

function builderPoints(e) {
  const obj = BUILDERS[e.builder](e.params || {});
  obj.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const pts = []; let tris = 0;
  obj.traverse((o) => {
    if (!o.isMesh || o.material.isShaderMaterial) return; // skip outline hulls
    const pos = o.geometry.attributes.position;
    tris += (o.geometry.index ? o.geometry.index.count : pos.count) / 3;
    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(pos, i).applyMatrix4(o.matrixWorld));
  });
  return { pts, tris };
}

const out = {};
for (const e of CATALOG) {
  validateEntry(e);
  const r = e.source === 'mesh' ? await meshPoints(e) : builderPoints(e);
  if (!r) { console.warn('MISSING', e.id, e.path); continue; }
  const { pts, tris } = r;
  const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(pts);
  const ground = pts.filter((p) => p.y < box.min.y + (e.groundY ?? 0.6));
  const g = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(ground);
  let roof = null;
  if (e.eaveY != null) {
    const rb = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(pts.filter((p) => p.y >= e.eaveY));
    roof = { min: rb.min.toArray(), max: rb.max.toArray() };
  }
  const r3 = (a) => a.map((v) => Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000);
  out[e.id] = {
    tris: Math.round(tris),
// Town validation — pure 2D math over a compiled town (no three.js). Returns
// { ok, checks:[{ name, ok, failures:[{msg,a,b}], warn? }], stats }. `ok` is
// true when every non-warn check passes. Rotated-OBB SAT is used throughout so
// placement is judged on the real rotated footprints, not axis-aligned boxes.
import { applyDefaults } from './spec.js';
import { math as Math } from './math.js';
import { CORE_KINDS, rectForInst } from './compile.js';
import {
  obbFromRect, rectXZ, obbCorners, obbOverlap, obbSeparation, obbPointDistance,
  segPointDistance, strutEnds, toWorldXZ, pointInArea, norm, dist, rotDir,
} from './geometry.js';

const place = (inst) => ({ x: inst.pos[0], z: inst.pos[1], rotY: inst.rotY });

function footprintObb(inst, catalog) {
  const e = catalog.get(inst.id);
  return obbFromRect(e.footprint, place(inst), inst.scale || 1);
}
function boundsObb(inst, catalog) {
  return obbFromRect(rectForInst(inst, catalog), place(inst), inst.scale || 1);
}
/** World [x,z] -> local [x,z] under a placement (inverse of toWorldXZ). */
function localOf([wx, wz], pl) {
  const dx = wx - pl.x, dz = wz - pl.z, c = Math.cos(pl.rotY || 0), s = Math.sin(pl.rotY || 0);
  return [dx * c - dz * s, dx * s + dz * c];
}

export function validate(town, catalog, spec) {
  const checks = [];
  const add = (name, failures, warn = false) => checks.push({ name, ok: failures.length === 0, failures, warn });
  const inst = town.instances;
  const area = town.area;
  const plazaR = (inst.find((i) => i.kind === 'plaza')?.radius) ?? spec.plaza.radius;
  const ringR = spec.plaza.ring.radius, ringW = spec.plaza.ring.width;
  const ringInner = ringR - ringW / 2, ringOuter = ringR + ringW / 2;
  const clearR = Math.max(plazaR, ringOuter); // keep-clear radius for buildings

  const buildings = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'house' || i.kind === 'landmark');
  const houses = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'house');
  const paths = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'path');
  const fences = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'fence');
  const scatter = inst.filter((i) => ['tree', 'bush', 'prop'].includes(i.kind));
  const solids = inst.filter((i) => ['house', 'landmark', 'prop', 'tree', 'bush', 'well'].includes(i.kind));

  // 1. footprint-overlap — no two solid footprints may overlap.
  {
    const f = [];
    const obbs = solids.map((i) => footprintObb(i, catalog));
    for (let a = 0; a < solids.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < solids.length; b++) {
      if (obbOverlap(obbs[a], obbs[b])) f.push({ msg: `${solids[a].id} overlaps ${solids[b].id}`, a: solids[a].pos, b: solids[b].pos });
    }
    add('footprint-overlap', f);
  }

  // 2. roof-overlap — building roof boxes (XZ) may not intersect.
  {
    const f = [];
    const rhs = buildings.map((h) => { const e = catalog.get(h.id); return e.roof ? obbFromRect(rectXZ(e.roof), place(h), h.scale || 1) : null; });
    for (let a = 0; a < buildings.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < buildings.length; b++) {
      if (rhs[a] && rhs[b] && obbOverlap(rhs[a], rhs[b])) f.push({ msg: `roof ${buildings[a].id} overlaps ${buildings[b].id}`, a: buildings[a].pos, b: buildings[b].pos });
    }
    add('roof-overlap', f);
  }

  // 3. setback — lots apart (gap >= 0) and footprints >= 1 off the ring/plaza.
  {
    const f = [];
    const lots = buildings.map((h) => obbFromRect(catalog.get(h.id).lot || catalog.get(h.id).footprint, place(h), h.scale || 1));
    for (let a = 0; a < buildings.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < buildings.length; b++) {
      if (obbSeparation(lots[a], lots[b]) < -0.01) f.push({ msg: `lots overlap ${buildings[a].id}/${buildings[b].id}`, a: buildings[a].pos, b: buildings[b].pos });
    }
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const d = obbPointDistance(footprintObb(h, catalog), [0, 0]) - clearR;
      if (d < 1 - 0.01) f.push({ msg: `${h.id} only ${d.toFixed(2)} from plaza/ring`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('setback', f);
  }

  // 4. trees-off-paths — trees/bushes clear of every path centreline.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const s of scatter.filter((i) => i.kind !== 'prop')) {
      const e = catalog.get(s.id);
      const rad = Math.max(e.footprint.max[0] - e.footprint.min[0], e.footprint.max[1] - e.footprint.min[1]) / 2 * (s.scale || 1);
      for (const p of paths) {
        const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
        if (segPointDistance(A, B, s.pos) < p.params.width / 2 + rad - 0.01) { f.push({ msg: `${s.id} on path`, a: s.pos }); break; }
      }
    }
    add('trees-off-paths', f);
  }

  // 5. trees-outside-lots — trees/bushes never inside a building lot.
  {
    const f = [];
    const lots = buildings.map((h) => obbFromRect(catalog.get(h.id).lot || catalog.get(h.id).footprint, place(h), h.scale || 1));
    for (const s of scatter.filter((i) => i.kind !== 'prop')) {
      if (lots.some((l) => obbPointDistance(l, s.pos) <= 0)) f.push({ msg: `${s.id} inside a lot`, a: s.pos });
    }
    add('trees-outside-lots', f);
  }

  // 6. door-reachable — each building's path snap meets a path end, and that
  //    path's other end lands in the plaza RING band (ring connectivity then
  //    links every door to the plaza).
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const snap = e.pathSnaps?.[0];
      if (!snap) { f.push({ msg: `${h.id} has no path snap`, a: h.pos }); continue; }
      const sp = toWorldXZ([snap.pos[0], snap.pos[2]], place(h));
      let reached = false;
      for (const p of paths.filter((p) => p.lot === h.lot)) {
        const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
        const dA = dist(A, sp), dB = dist(B, sp);
        const plazaEnd = dA < dB ? B : A;
        const nearHouse = Math.min(dA, dB);
        const rEnd = Math.hypot(plazaEnd[0], plazaEnd[1]);
        if (nearHouse <= 0.6 && rEnd >= ringInner - 0.4 && rEnd <= ringOuter + 0.4) { reached = true; break; }
      }
      if (!reached) f.push({ msg: `${h.id} door path does not reach the plaza ring`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('door-reachable', f);
  }

  // 7. within-area — every instance's rotated bounds corners inside the area.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const i of inst) {
      if (i.kind === 'plaza') { if (i.radius > (area.shape === 'rect' ? Math.min(area.w, area.d) / 2 : area.radius)) f.push({ msg: 'plaza exceeds area', a: [0, 0] }); continue; }
      if (!catalog.get(i.id)) continue;
      if (obbCorners(boundsObb(i, catalog)).some((c) => !pointInArea(area, c, 0))) f.push({ msg: `${i.id} pokes outside the area`, a: i.pos });
    }
    add('within-area', f);
  }

  // 8. fences-not-on-paths — fence segments clear of every path.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const s of fences) {
      const o = footprintObb(s, catalog);
      if (paths.some((p) => obbOverlap(o, obbFromRect(rectForInst(p, catalog), place(p))))) f.push({ msg: `fence on a path`, a: s.pos });
    }
    add('fences-not-on-paths', f);
  }

  // 9/10. fence continuity + gate alignment. A fenced lot's perimeter (minus
  //       the front gate) must have no gap > 0.5, and the front gate must sit
  //       on the door path.
  {
    const contFails = [];
    const gateFails = [];
    const pathWidth = spec.paths.width;
    const gate = pathWidth + 0.6;
    const byLot = new Map();
    for (const s of fences) { if (!byLot.has(s.lot)) byLot.set(s.lot, []); byLot.get(s.lot).push(s); }
    for (const [lotIdx, segs] of byLot) {
      const h = houses.find((x) => x.lot === lotIdx);
      if (!h) continue;
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const lot = e.lot || e.footprint;
      const pl = place(h);
      const seg0 = segs[0].seg || 3.0;
      // Perimeter runs (local coords). Front split around the centred gate.
      const runs = [
        ['back', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]]],
        ['left', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['right', [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]], [-gate / 2, lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [gate / 2, lot.max[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
      ];
      for (const [edge, la, lb] of runs) {
        const c1 = toWorldXZ(la, pl), c2 = toWorldXZ(lb, pl);
        const L = dist(c1, c2);
        if (L < 0.6) continue; // sub-run intentionally omitted
        const dir = norm([c2[0] - c1[0], c2[1] - c1[1]]);
        // Covered intervals from segments whose centre lies near this run.
        const iv = [];
        for (const s of segs) {
          const t = (s.pos[0] - c1[0]) * dir[0] + (s.pos[1] - c1[1]) * dir[1];
          const nd = Math.abs((s.pos[0] - c1[0]) * dir[1] - (s.pos[1] - c1[1]) * dir[0]);
          if (nd > 0.6 || t < -seg0 || t > L + seg0) continue; // not on this run
          iv.push([t - seg0 / 2, t + seg0 / 2]);
        }
        const gap = maxGap(iv, L, 0.5); // ignore 0.5 tolerance at the run ends
        if (gap > 0.5) contFails.push({ msg: `${h.id} fence gap ${gap.toFixed(2)} on ${edge}`, a: h.pos });
      }
      // Gate: no front fence may cover the door-path crossing (local x ~ 0).
      const frontSegs = segs.filter((s) => Math.abs(localOf(s.pos, pl)[1] - lot.max[1]) < 0.6);
      const blocks = frontSegs.some((s) => {
        const lx = localOf(s.pos, pl)[0];
        return Math.abs(lx) - seg0 / 2 < pathWidth / 2 - 0.05; // seg overlaps the gate corridor
      });
      if (blocks) gateFails.push({ msg: `${h.id} fence blocks its front gate`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('fence-continuity', contFails);
    add('fence-gate-aligned', gateFails);
  }

  // gate-at-door — verify explicit gates and the actual opening on the fence line.
  {
    const f = [];
    const gates = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'gate');
    const fenced = buildings.filter((h) => h.lot != null && fences.some((s) => s.lot === h.lot && s.edge === 'front'));
    for (const g of gates) {
      if (!fenced.some((h) => h.lot === g.lot)) f.push({ msg: `orphan gate instance ${inst.indexOf(g)} lot ${g.lot}`, a: g.pos });
    }
    for (const h of fenced) {
      const label = `instance ${inst.indexOf(h)} ${h.id} lot ${h.lot}`;
      const gs = gates.filter((g) => g.lot === h.lot);
      if (gs.length !== 1 || gs[0]?.edge !== 'front') {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} needs exactly one front gate (found ${gs.length})`, a: h.pos });
        continue;
      }
      const g = gs[0], pl = place(h);
      const e = catalog.get(h.id), lot = e.lot || e.footprint;
      const frontZ = lot.max[1] * (h.scale || 1);
      const [gx, gz] = localOf(g.pos, pl);
      if (!(Math.abs(gz - frontZ) <= 0.35)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate off front fence line`, a: g.pos });
      const lotPaths = paths.filter((p) => p.lot === h.lot);
      const p = lotPaths[0];
      if (lotPaths.length !== 1) f.push({ msg: `${label} needs one door path (found ${lotPaths.length})`, a: g.pos });
      if (p) {
        const d = rotDir([0, 1], p.rotY);
        const lateral = Math.abs((g.pos[0] - p.pos[0]) * d[1] - (g.pos[1] - p.pos[1]) * d[0]);
        if (!(lateral <= 0.25)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate ${lateral.toFixed(2)} off door path centreline`, a: g.pos });
        const width = (p.params?.width ?? p.width ?? spec.paths.width) * (p.scale || 1);
        if (!(g.width * (g.scale || 1) >= width)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate narrower than door path (${g.width} < ${width})`, a: g.pos });
      }
      // A zero-depth OBB tests the opening interval on the exact front line.
      // Use fence footprints, including rotation and scale; touching endpoints
      // are allowed, but any positive intersection closes part of the gap.
      if (Number.isFinite(g.width) && g.width > 0) {
        const half = g.width * (g.scale || 1) / 2;
        const opening = obbFromRect({ min: [gx - half, frontZ], max: [gx + half, frontZ] }, pl);
        if (fences.some((s) => obbOverlap(opening, footprintObb(s, catalog), -1e-6))) {
          f.push({ msg: `${label} gate opening overlaps a fence segment`, a: g.pos });
        }
      } else f.push({ msg: `${label} gate has invalid width`, a: g.pos });

  };

  function scatter(cfg, kind, mult, { jitter: jit = false } = {}) {
    const list = queryCatalog(catalog, cfg.pick);
    if (!list.length) return;
    const [lo, hi] = scaleCount(cfg.count, mult);
    const n = rng.int(lo, hi);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      for (let t = 0; t < spec.retries.placement; t++) {
        const entry = rng.pick(list);
        const scale = jit ? rng.float(0.9, 1.15) : 1;
        const pos = sampleArea();
        const rad = fpRadius(entry, scale);
        if (!pointInArea(area, pos, boundsRadius(entry, scale) + 0.3)) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) < clearR + rad + 0.5) continue; // off plaza + ring
        if (cfg.maxRadius && Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) > cfg.maxRadius) continue; // keep dressing near the town
        if (insideLot(pos, rad)) continue;
        if (kind === 'tree') {
          if (inViewCorridor(pos)) continue;
          if (Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) < houseRing[0]) continue; // keep the green open
          if (!offPaths(pos, rad + 2.0)) continue;
        }
        if (!offPaths(pos, rad)) continue;
        if (scattered.some((s) => Math.hypot(s.pos[0] - pos[0], s.pos[1] - pos[1]) < s.radius + rad + 0.6)) continue;
        scattered.push({ pos, radius: rad });
        instances.push({ id: entry.id, kind, pos, rotY: rng.float(0, TAU), scale });
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  scatter(spec.trees, 'tree', spec.density.trees, { jitter: true });
  scatter(spec.bushes, 'bush', spec.density.bushes, { jitter: true });

  // Props: near a house (inside/edge of its lot), off footprint and paths.
  if (houses.length) {
    const list = queryCatalog(catalog, spec.props.pick);
    if (list.length) {
      const [lo, hi] = scaleCount(spec.props.count, spec.density.props);
      const n = rng.int(lo, hi);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        for (let t = 0; t < spec.retries.placement; t++) {
          const entry = rng.pick(list);
          const h = rng.pick(houses);
          const rad = fpRadius(entry);
          const lx = rng.sign() * rng.float(1.6, Math.max(1.7, (h.entry.footprint.max[0]) + 1.2));
          const lz = rng.float(h.entry.footprint.min[1] + 0.3, h.entry.footprint.max[1] - 0.3);
          const pos = toWorldXZ([lx, lz], { x: h.pos[0], z: h.pos[1], rotY: h.rotY });
          const fpO = obbFromRect(h.entry.footprint, { x: h.pos[0], z: h.pos[1], rotY: h.rotY });
          if (!pointInArea(area, pos, rad + 0.3)) continue;
          if (obbPointDistance(fpO, pos) < rad + 0.2) continue;
          if (Math.hypot(pos[0], pos[1]) < clearR + rad) continue;
          if (!offPaths(pos, rad)) continue;
          if (scattered.some((s) => Math.hypot(s.pos[0] - pos[0], s.pos[1] - pos[1]) < s.radius + rad + 0.3)) continue;
          scattered.push({ pos, radius: rad });
          instances.push({ id: entry.id, kind: 'prop', pos, rotY: rng.float(0, TAU), scale: 1, lot: h.index });
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const camera = computeCamera(instances, buildings, catalog);
  // Facing is recorded against the camera actually suggested for this layout.
  const camGround = [camera.pos[0], camera.pos[2]];
  for (const b of buildings) b.inst.facing = facingAngles(b.pos, b.rotY, camGround);
  return { name: spec.name, seed, attempt, grow: +grow.toFixed(3), area, camera, instances, camHint: camXZ.map((v) => +v.toFixed(2)),
    facingArc: [+(arcLo * DEG).toFixed(1), +(arcHi * DEG).toFixed(1)], buildingsWanted: total, placementRejects: rejects };
}

// Rank offsets before adding the absolute angle. Symmetric slots have the
// same priority; explicitly prefer the lower-angle slot on ties. Subtracting
// AWAY_ANGLE from absolute angles made ulp noise reverse their order in Chrome.
export function buildingSlots(total, arcLo, arcHi, center) {
  return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, index) => {
    const offset = -arcLo + ((index + 0.5) / total) * (arcLo + arcHi);
    return { angle: center + offset, rank: Math.round(Math.abs(offset) * 1e12), index };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank || a.index - b.index).map((s) => s.angle);
}

const worstCameraAngle = (town) => town.instances.reduce((m, i) => Math.max(m, i.facing ? i.facing.cameraAngle : 0), 0);

// --- camera suggestion from the final layout -------------------------------
const norm3 = (v) => { const l = Math.hypot(v[0], v[1], v[2]) || 1; return [v[0] / l, v[1] / l, v[2] / l]; };
function computeCamera(instances, buildings, catalog) {
  // Mean outward direction the doors look (= where the camera should stand).
  let fx = 0, fz = 0;
  for (const b of buildings) { const f = rotDir([0, 1], b.rotY); fx += f[0]; fz += f[1]; }
  const dir = (fx || fz) ? norm([fx, fz]) : VIEWER_DIR;

  // Core sample points (ground + top) over every CORE instance.
  const vfov = CAMERA_FOV * Math.PI / 180;
  let el = CAMERA_ELEV_DEG * Math.PI / 180; // searched up to CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG below
  const targetY = 2.5;
  const core = [];
  for (const inst of instances) {
    if (!CORE_KINDS.has(inst.kind) || (inst.id !== '__plaza' && !catalog.get(inst.id))) continue;
    const rect = rectForInst(inst, catalog);
    const e = catalog.get(inst.id);
    const yTop = (inst.kind === 'ring') ? 0.1 : ((e?.bounds.max[1] || 0) * (inst.scale || 1));
    for (const c of obbCorners(obbFromRect(rect, { x: inst.pos[0], z: inst.pos[1], rotY: inst.rotY }, inst.scale || 1))) {
      core.push([c[0], 0, c[1]], [c[0], yTop, c[1]]);
    }
  }
  // Target = centroid of the core XZ bounds.
  const center = [0, 0];
  if (core.length) {
    let a = Infinity, b = -Infinity, c = Infinity, d = -Infinity;
    for (const p of core) { a = Math.min(a, p[0]); b = Math.max(b, p[0]); c = Math.min(c, p[2]); d = Math.max(d, p[2]); }
    center[0] = (a + b) / 2; center[1] = (c + d) / 2;
  }

  // Fit against an EFFECTIVE viewport: the left PANEL_LEFT_CUT of the frame is
  // reserved for the panel, so require the core to sit in screen x in
  // [PANEL_LEFT_CUT, 1] (normalised nx in [leftLim, 1]).
  const tanV = Math.tan(vfov / 2), tanH = tanV * 16 / 9;
  const leftLim = -(1 - 2 * PANEL_LEFT_CUT);
  // Project the core from a camera at slant distance D looking at `tgt`.
  // Returns normalised screen extents (nx, ny in [-1, 1] = frame edge).
  const extents = (D, tgt) => {
    const eye = [tgt[0] + dir[0] * D * Math.cos(el), targetY + D * Math.sin(el), tgt[1] + dir[1] * D * Math.cos(el)];
    const fwd = norm3([tgt[0] - eye[0], targetY - eye[1], tgt[1] - eye[2]]);
    const right = norm3([-fwd[2], 0, fwd[0]]);
    const up = [right[1] * fwd[2] - right[2] * fwd[1], right[2] * fwd[0] - right[0] * fwd[2], right[0] * fwd[1] - right[1] * fwd[0]];
    let x0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y0 = Infinity, y1 = -Infinity, behind = false;
    for (const p of core) {
      const v = [p[0] - eye[0], p[1] - eye[1], p[2] - eye[2]];
      const zc = v[0] * fwd[0] + v[1] * fwd[1] + v[2] * fwd[2];
      if (zc <= 0) { behind = true; break; }
      const nx = (v[0] * right[0] + v[1] * right[1] + v[2] * right[2]) / zc / tanH;
      const ny = (v[0] * up[0] + v[1] * up[1] + v[2] * up[2]) / zc / tanV;
      x0 = Math.min(x0, nx); x1 = Math.max(x1, nx); y0 = Math.min(y0, ny); y1 = Math.max(y1, ny);
    }
    return { behind, x0, x1, y0, y1, right };
  };
  // For each D, slide the target so the core is centred in the effective
  // viewport (x in [leftLim, 1], y in [-1, 1]), then bisect for the smallest
  // D at which the core spans <= CAMERA_FILL of that viewport on both axes
  // (the binding axis then fills ~CAMERA_FILL, a small margin either side).
  const wantX = (leftLim + 1) / 2;
  const groundFwd = [-dir[0], -dir[1]];
  const fitAt = (D) => {
    let tgt = [...center];
    let ex = extents(D, tgt);
    for (let k = 0; k < 6 && !ex.behind; k++) {
      const sx = ((ex.x0 + ex.x1) / 2 - wantX) * tanH * D;
      const sy = ((ex.y0 + ex.y1) / 2) * tanV * D / Math.sin(el);
      tgt = [tgt[0] + ex.right[0] * sx + groundFwd[0] * sy, tgt[1] + ex.right[2] * sx + groundFwd[1] * sy];
      ex = extents(D, tgt);
    }
    const fx = ex.behind ? Infinity : (ex.x1 - ex.x0) / (1 - leftLim);
    const fy = ex.behind ? Infinity : (ex.y1 - ex.y0) / 2;
    const inside = !ex.behind && ex.x0 >= leftLim && ex.x1 <= 1 && ex.y0 >= -1 && ex.y1 <= 1;
    return { tgt, fx, fy, ok: inside && Math.max(fx, fy) <= CAMERA_FILL };
  };
  const solve = () => {
    let lo = 8, hi = 220, best = fitAt(hi);
    for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) {
      const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
      const r = fitAt(mid);
      if (r.ok) { hi = mid; best = r; } else lo = mid;
    }
    return { D: hi, ...best };
  };
  // A wide arc makes the fit width-limited, leaving empty ground below the
  // town. Raising the elevation (up to CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG) spreads the core
  // vertically: pick the lowest elevation whose minor-axis fill is within
  // 0.01 of the best (the binding axis is CAMERA_FILL at every elevation).
  const cands = [];
  for (let deg = CAMERA_ELEV_DEG; deg <= CAMERA_ELEV_MAX_DEG + 1e-9; deg += 1) {
    el = deg * Math.PI / 180;
    cands.push({ deg, el, ...solve() });
  }
  const bestMinor = Math.max(...cands.map((c) => Math.min(c.fx, c.fy)));
  const pick = cands.find((c) => Math.min(c.fx, c.fy) >= bestMinor - 0.01);
  el = pick.el;
  const D = +pick.D.toFixed(2);
  const tgt = pick.tgt;
  center[0] = tgt[0]; center[1] = tgt[1];
  const horiz = D * Math.cos(el), vert = D * Math.sin(el);
  // A rough radius for callers that want it.
  let radius = 1;
  for (const p of core) radius = Math.max(radius, Math.hypot(p[0] - center[0], p[2] - center[1]));
  return {
    pos: [center[0] + dir[0] * horiz, targetY + vert, center[1] + dir[1] * horiz],
    target: [center[0], targetY, center[1]],
    dir, distance: D, fov: CAMERA_FOV, elevation: pick.deg, radius, center, leftCut: PANEL_LEFT_CUT, fill: CAMERA_FILL,
    fillX: +pick.fx.toFixed(3), fillY: +pick.fy.toFixed(3),
  };
}

// --- town-level retry loop -------------------------------------------------
// The facing caps are HARD. checks.js (owned separately) may grow its own
// facing check; until it does, the compiler appends 'facing-caps' so a town
// whose recorded facing breaks houses.facing is retried, not accepted.
function addFacingCheck(town, spec) {
  if (town.report.checks.some((c) => /facing/.test(c.name))) return;
  const { maxCameraAngle, maxPlazaAngle } = spec.houses.facing;
  const failures = [];
  for (const i of town.instances) {
    if (!i.facing) continue;
    if (i.facing.cameraAngle > maxCameraAngle || i.facing.plazaAngle > maxPlazaAngle) {
      failures.push({ msg: `${i.id} faces ${i.facing.cameraAngle} deg off camera / ${i.facing.plazaAngle} off plaza`, a: i.pos });
    }
  }
  town.report.checks.push({ name: 'facing-caps', ok: failures.length === 0, failures, warn: false });
  town.report.ok = town.report.ok && failures.length === 0;
}

const failScore = (report) => report.checks.reduce((n, c) => n + (c.warn ? 0 : (c.failures?.length || 0)), 0);

export function compileTown(rawSpec, catalog, { seed } = {}) {
  const spec = applyDefaults(rawSpec);
  const gateErr = gateSpecError(spec, catalog);
  if (gateErr) throw new Error(`spec: ${gateErr}`);
  const baseSeed = (seed ?? spec.seed ?? 1) >>> 0;
  const townRetries = spec.retries.town;
  let best = null;
  let grow = 1;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= townRetries; attempt++) {
    const subSeed = attempt === 0 ? baseSeed : deriveSeed(baseSeed, attempt);
    // Door yaws are chosen against a camera guess; the real camera comes from
    // the finished layout. If the recorded camera angles overshoot the cap,
    // redo the layout (same sub-seed) against that camera, keeping the best.
    const cap = spec.houses.facing.maxCameraAngle;
    let town = buildOnce(spec, catalog, subSeed, attempt, grow);
    for (let k = 0; k < 3 && worstCameraAngle(town) > cap; k++) {
      const again = buildOnce(spec, catalog, subSeed, attempt, grow, [town.camera.pos[0], town.camera.pos[2]]);
      if (worstCameraAngle(again) <= worstCameraAngle(town)) town = again;
      else break;
    }
    town.seed = baseSeed;       // the requested seed (subSeed is the attempt's)
    town.subSeed = subSeed;
    town.report = validate(town, catalog, spec);
    addFacingCheck(town, spec);
    town.ok = town.report.ok;
    if (town.ok) return town;
    if (!best || failScore(town.report) < failScore(best.report)) best = town;
    // Auto-grow the extent when buildings can't all fit (house-count fail).
    const hc = town.report.checks.find((c) => c.name === 'house-count-in-range');
    if (hc && !hc.ok) grow = Math.min(1.7, grow * 1.12);
  }
  best.ok = false;
  return best;
}

for (const perturb of [false, true]) {
  let perturbApplied = false;
  // Vite 5 opens an HMR socket even with hmr:false. Give it an unlistened
  // HTTP server so middleware/SSR tests also work in restricted environments.
  const hmrServer = createHttpServer();
  const server = await createServer({
    root,
    server: { middlewareMode: true, hmr: { server: hmrServer }, watch: null },
    appType: 'custom',
    plugins: perturb ? [{
      name: 'parity-engine-rounding',
      enforce: 'pre',
      transform(code, id) {
        if (id === `${root}src/compiler/math.js`) {
          perturbApplied = true;
          // Simulate a native libm result differing by ~one ulp. Before the
          // fix this reproduced BOTH reported Chrome stats exactly (7/1234).
          return `const Math = Object.create(globalThis.Math);
            for (const name of ['sin', 'cos', 'tan', 'asin', 'atan', 'atan2', 'hypot']) {
              Math[name] = (...args) => globalThis.Math[name](...args) * (1 + Number.EPSILON);
            }
            ${code}`;
        }
      },
    }] : [],
  });
  try {
    const { RESOLVED } = await server.ssrLoadModule('/src/catalog/loader.js');
    const { default: hamlet } = await server.ssrLoadModule('/specs/hamlet.json');
    const viteSpec = await server.ssrLoadModule('/src/compiler/spec.js');
    const viteCompiler = await server.ssrLoadModule('/src/compiler/compile.js');
    if (perturb) assert(perturbApplied, 'engine rounding simulation must be applied');
    const browserSpec = viteSpec.validateSpec(hamlet, RESOLVED);
    assert.equal(json([...RESOLVED]), json([...catalog]), 'resolved catalog parity');
    assert.equal(json(browserSpec), json(spec), 'validated spec parity');
    const snapshot = json([hamlet, browserSpec, [...RESOLVED]]);
    // Repeat in reverse order with the same objects to catch mutation/caches.
    for (const seed of [...seeds, ...seeds.toReversed()]) {
      const actual = json(viteCompiler.compileTown(browserSpec, RESOLVED, { seed }));
      assert.equal(actual, expected.get(seed), `seed ${seed}: full town JSON (${perturb ? 'perturbed math' : 'Vite SSR'})`);
      assert.equal(json(compileTown(spec, catalog, { seed })), expected.get(seed), `seed ${seed}: Node repeat`);
    }
    assert.equal(json([hamlet, browserSpec, [...RESOLVED]]), snapshot, 'Vite inputs must not mutate');
    console.log(`parity ${perturb ? 'Vite SSR + one-ulp math perturbation' : 'Vite SSR'}: ${seeds.length} seeds, full JSON identical (forward + reverse)`);
  } finally {
    await server.close();
  }
}

t = base(); t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'tree').pos = [60, 0]; expect('tree outside area', t, 'within-area');
t = base(); const tr2 = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'tree'); tr2.pos = [...t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'house').pos]; expect('tree in lot', t, 'trees-outside-lots');

// --- v2 breaks -------------------------------------------------------------
// Path dead-ends off the ring: shove a path's plaza end well past the ring.
t = base(); const pd = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'path'); pd.pos = [pd.pos[0] * 1.8, pd.pos[1] * 1.8]; expect('path dead-ends off ring', t, 'door-reachable');
// Fence across a door path: drop a fence segment onto a path centre.
t = base(); const fseg = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'fence'); const pp = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'path'); fseg.pos = [...pp.pos]; fseg.rotY = pp.rotY + Math.PI / 2; expect('fence across a door path', t, 'fences-not-on-paths');
// Landmark overlapping a house: move the landmark onto a house.
t = base(); const lm = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'landmark'); const hh = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'house'); lm.pos = [...hh.pos]; expect('landmark on a house', t, 'footprint-overlap');

// --- v3 breaks -------------------------------------------------------------
t = base(); t.instances.splice(t.instances.findIndex(i => i.kind === 'gate'), 1); expect('delete a gate', t, 'gate-at-door');
t = base(); const shiftedGate = t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'gate');
const gateHouse = t.instances.find(i => (i.kind === 'house' || i.kind === 'landmark') && i.lot === shiftedGate.lot);
shiftedGate.pos[0] += 1.2 * Math.cos(gateHouse.rotY); shiftedGate.pos[1] -= 1.2 * Math.sin(gateHouse.rotY);
expect('shift gate along fence', t, 'gate-at-door');
t = base(); t.instances.push({ ...structuredClone(t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'gate')), lot: 'orphan', pos: [0, 0] }); expect('orphan gate', t, 'gate-at-door');
t = base(); t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'house').rotY += Math.PI / 2; expect('house side-on', t, 'house-facing');
t = base(); t.instances.find(i => i.kind === 'house').rotY += Math.PI; expect('house back to plaza', t, 'house-facing');

if (bad) { console.error(bad + ' break(s) not caught'); process.exit(1); }
console.log('all breaks caught');

/bin/bash -lc 'cat src/compiler/checks.js' in /workspace/cel-town
 succeeded in 0ms:
// Town validation — pure 2D math over a compiled town (no three.js). Returns
// { ok, checks:[{ name, ok, failures:[{msg,a,b}], warn? }], stats }. `ok` is
// true when every non-warn check passes. Rotated-OBB SAT is used throughout so
// placement is judged on the real rotated footprints, not axis-aligned boxes.
import { applyDefaults } from './spec.js';
import { math as Math } from './math.js';
import { CORE_KINDS, rectForInst } from './compile.js';
import {
  obbFromRect, rectXZ, obbCorners, obbOverlap, obbSeparation, obbPointDistance,
  segPointDistance, strutEnds, toWorldXZ, pointInArea, norm, dist, rotDir,
} from './geometry.js';

const place = (inst) => ({ x: inst.pos[0], z: inst.pos[1], rotY: inst.rotY });

function footprintObb(inst, catalog) {
  const e = catalog.get(inst.id);
  return obbFromRect(e.footprint, place(inst), inst.scale || 1);
}
function boundsObb(inst, catalog) {
  return obbFromRect(rectForInst(inst, catalog), place(inst), inst.scale || 1);
}
/** World [x,z] -> local [x,z] under a placement (inverse of toWorldXZ). */
function localOf([wx, wz], pl) {
  const dx = wx - pl.x, dz = wz - pl.z, c = Math.cos(pl.rotY || 0), s = Math.sin(pl.rotY || 0);
  return [dx * c - dz * s, dx * s + dz * c];
}

export function validate(town, catalog, spec) {
  const checks = [];
  const add = (name, failures, warn = false) => checks.push({ name, ok: failures.length === 0, failures, warn });
  const inst = town.instances;
  const area = town.area;
  const plazaR = (inst.find((i) => i.kind === 'plaza')?.radius) ?? spec.plaza.radius;
  const ringR = spec.plaza.ring.radius, ringW = spec.plaza.ring.width;
  const ringInner = ringR - ringW / 2, ringOuter = ringR + ringW / 2;
  const clearR = Math.max(plazaR, ringOuter); // keep-clear radius for buildings

  const buildings = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'house' || i.kind === 'landmark');
  const houses = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'house');
  const paths = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'path');
  const fences = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'fence');
  const scatter = inst.filter((i) => ['tree', 'bush', 'prop'].includes(i.kind));
  const solids = inst.filter((i) => ['house', 'landmark', 'prop', 'tree', 'bush', 'well'].includes(i.kind));

  // 1. footprint-overlap — no two solid footprints may overlap.
  {
    const f = [];
    const obbs = solids.map((i) => footprintObb(i, catalog));
    for (let a = 0; a < solids.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < solids.length; b++) {
      if (obbOverlap(obbs[a], obbs[b])) f.push({ msg: `${solids[a].id} overlaps ${solids[b].id}`, a: solids[a].pos, b: solids[b].pos });
    }
    add('footprint-overlap', f);
  }

  // 2. roof-overlap — building roof boxes (XZ) may not intersect.
  {
    const f = [];
    const rhs = buildings.map((h) => { const e = catalog.get(h.id); return e.roof ? obbFromRect(rectXZ(e.roof), place(h), h.scale || 1) : null; });
    for (let a = 0; a < buildings.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < buildings.length; b++) {
      if (rhs[a] && rhs[b] && obbOverlap(rhs[a], rhs[b])) f.push({ msg: `roof ${buildings[a].id} overlaps ${buildings[b].id}`, a: buildings[a].pos, b: buildings[b].pos });
    }
    add('roof-overlap', f);
  }

  // 3. setback — lots apart (gap >= 0) and footprints >= 1 off the ring/plaza.
  {
    const f = [];
    const lots = buildings.map((h) => obbFromRect(catalog.get(h.id).lot || catalog.get(h.id).footprint, place(h), h.scale || 1));
    for (let a = 0; a < buildings.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < buildings.length; b++) {
      if (obbSeparation(lots[a], lots[b]) < -0.01) f.push({ msg: `lots overlap ${buildings[a].id}/${buildings[b].id}`, a: buildings[a].pos, b: buildings[b].pos });
    }
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const d = obbPointDistance(footprintObb(h, catalog), [0, 0]) - clearR;
      if (d < 1 - 0.01) f.push({ msg: `${h.id} only ${d.toFixed(2)} from plaza/ring`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('setback', f);
  }

  // 4. trees-off-paths — trees/bushes clear of every path centreline.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const s of scatter.filter((i) => i.kind !== 'prop')) {
      const e = catalog.get(s.id);
      const rad = Math.max(e.footprint.max[0] - e.footprint.min[0], e.footprint.max[1] - e.footprint.min[1]) / 2 * (s.scale || 1);
      for (const p of paths) {
        const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
        if (segPointDistance(A, B, s.pos) < p.params.width / 2 + rad - 0.01) { f.push({ msg: `${s.id} on path`, a: s.pos }); break; }
      }
    }
    add('trees-off-paths', f);
  }

  // 5. trees-outside-lots — trees/bushes never inside a building lot.
  {
    const f = [];
    const lots = buildings.map((h) => obbFromRect(catalog.get(h.id).lot || catalog.get(h.id).footprint, place(h), h.scale || 1));
    for (const s of scatter.filter((i) => i.kind !== 'prop')) {
      if (lots.some((l) => obbPointDistance(l, s.pos) <= 0)) f.push({ msg: `${s.id} inside a lot`, a: s.pos });
    }
    add('trees-outside-lots', f);
  }

  // 6. door-reachable — each building's path snap meets a path end, and that
  //    path's other end lands in the plaza RING band (ring connectivity then
  //    links every door to the plaza).
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const snap = e.pathSnaps?.[0];
      if (!snap) { f.push({ msg: `${h.id} has no path snap`, a: h.pos }); continue; }
      const sp = toWorldXZ([snap.pos[0], snap.pos[2]], place(h));
      let reached = false;
      for (const p of paths.filter((p) => p.lot === h.lot)) {
        const [A, B] = strutEnds(p);
        const dA = dist(A, sp), dB = dist(B, sp);
        const plazaEnd = dA < dB ? B : A;
        const nearHouse = Math.min(dA, dB);
        const rEnd = Math.hypot(plazaEnd[0], plazaEnd[1]);
        if (nearHouse <= 0.6 && rEnd >= ringInner - 0.4 && rEnd <= ringOuter + 0.4) { reached = true; break; }
      }
      if (!reached) f.push({ msg: `${h.id} door path does not reach the plaza ring`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('door-reachable', f);
  }

  // 7. within-area — every instance's rotated bounds corners inside the area.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const i of inst) {
      if (i.kind === 'plaza') { if (i.radius > (area.shape === 'rect' ? Math.min(area.w, area.d) / 2 : area.radius)) f.push({ msg: 'plaza exceeds area', a: [0, 0] }); continue; }
      if (!catalog.get(i.id)) continue;
      if (obbCorners(boundsObb(i, catalog)).some((c) => !pointInArea(area, c, 0))) f.push({ msg: `${i.id} pokes outside the area`, a: i.pos });
    }
    add('within-area', f);
  }

  // 8. fences-not-on-paths — fence segments clear of every path.
  {
    const f = [];
    for (const s of fences) {
      const o = footprintObb(s, catalog);
      if (paths.some((p) => obbOverlap(o, obbFromRect(rectForInst(p, catalog), place(p))))) f.push({ msg: `fence on a path`, a: s.pos });
    }
    add('fences-not-on-paths', f);
  }

  // 9/10. fence continuity + gate alignment. A fenced lot's perimeter (minus
  //       the front gate) must have no gap > 0.5, and the front gate must sit
  //       on the door path.
  {
    const contFails = [];
    const gateFails = [];
    const pathWidth = spec.paths.width;
    const gate = pathWidth + 0.6;
    const byLot = new Map();
    for (const s of fences) { if (!byLot.has(s.lot)) byLot.set(s.lot, []); byLot.get(s.lot).push(s); }
    for (const [lotIdx, segs] of byLot) {
      const h = houses.find((x) => x.lot === lotIdx);
      if (!h) continue;
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const lot = e.lot || e.footprint;
      const pl = place(h);
      const seg0 = segs[0].seg || 3.0;
      // Perimeter runs (local coords). Front split around the centred gate.
      const runs = [
        ['back', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]]],
        ['left', [lot.min[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['right', [lot.max[0], lot.min[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [lot.min[0], lot.max[1]], [-gate / 2, lot.max[1]]],
        ['front', [gate / 2, lot.max[1]], [lot.max[0], lot.max[1]]],
      ];
      for (const [edge, la, lb] of runs) {
        const c1 = toWorldXZ(la, pl), c2 = toWorldXZ(lb, pl);
        const L = dist(c1, c2);
        if (L < 0.6) continue; // sub-run intentionally omitted
        const dir = norm([c2[0] - c1[0], c2[1] - c1[1]]);
        // Covered intervals from segments whose centre lies near this run.
        const iv = [];
        for (const s of segs) {
          const t = (s.pos[0] - c1[0]) * dir[0] + (s.pos[1] - c1[1]) * dir[1];
          const nd = Math.abs((s.pos[0] - c1[0]) * dir[1] - (s.pos[1] - c1[1]) * dir[0]);
          if (nd > 0.6 || t < -seg0 || t > L + seg0) continue; // not on this run
          iv.push([t - seg0 / 2, t + seg0 / 2]);
        }
        const gap = maxGap(iv, L, 0.5); // ignore 0.5 tolerance at the run ends
        if (gap > 0.5) contFails.push({ msg: `${h.id} fence gap ${gap.toFixed(2)} on ${edge}`, a: h.pos });
      }
      // Gate: no front fence may cover the door-path crossing (local x ~ 0).
      const frontSegs = segs.filter((s) => Math.abs(localOf(s.pos, pl)[1] - lot.max[1]) < 0.6);
      const blocks = frontSegs.some((s) => {
        const lx = localOf(s.pos, pl)[0];
        return Math.abs(lx) - seg0 / 2 < pathWidth / 2 - 0.05; // seg overlaps the gate corridor
      });
      if (blocks) gateFails.push({ msg: `${h.id} fence blocks its front gate`, a: h.pos });
    }
    add('fence-continuity', contFails);
    add('fence-gate-aligned', gateFails);
  }

  // gate-at-door — verify explicit gates and the actual opening on the fence line.
  {
    const f = [];
    const gates = inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'gate');
    const fenced = buildings.filter((h) => h.lot != null && fences.some((s) => s.lot === h.lot && s.edge === 'front'));
    for (const g of gates) {
      if (!fenced.some((h) => h.lot === g.lot)) f.push({ msg: `orphan gate instance ${inst.indexOf(g)} lot ${g.lot}`, a: g.pos });
    }
    for (const h of fenced) {
      const label = `instance ${inst.indexOf(h)} ${h.id} lot ${h.lot}`;
      const gs = gates.filter((g) => g.lot === h.lot);
      if (gs.length !== 1 || gs[0]?.edge !== 'front') {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} needs exactly one front gate (found ${gs.length})`, a: h.pos });
        continue;
      }
      const g = gs[0], pl = place(h);
      const e = catalog.get(h.id), lot = e.lot || e.footprint;
      const frontZ = lot.max[1] * (h.scale || 1);
      const [gx, gz] = localOf(g.pos, pl);
      if (!(Math.abs(gz - frontZ) <= 0.35)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate off front fence line`, a: g.pos });
      const lotPaths = paths.filter((p) => p.lot === h.lot);
      const p = lotPaths[0];
      if (lotPaths.length !== 1) f.push({ msg: `${label} needs one door path (found ${lotPaths.length})`, a: g.pos });
      if (p) {
        const d = rotDir([0, 1], p.rotY);
        const lateral = Math.abs((g.pos[0] - p.pos[0]) * d[1] - (g.pos[1] - p.pos[1]) * d[0]);
        if (!(lateral <= 0.25)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate ${lateral.toFixed(2)} off door path centreline`, a: g.pos });
        const width = (p.params?.width ?? p.width ?? spec.paths.width) * (p.scale || 1);
        if (!(g.width * (g.scale || 1) >= width)) f.push({ msg: `${label} gate narrower than door path (${g.width} < ${width})`, a: g.pos });
      }
      // A zero-depth OBB tests the opening interval on the exact front line.
      // Use fence footprints, including rotation and scale; touching endpoints
      // are allowed, but any positive intersection closes part of the gap.
      if (Number.isFinite(g.width) && g.width > 0) {
        const half = g.width * (g.scale || 1) / 2;
        const opening = obbFromRect({ min: [gx - half, frontZ], max: [gx + half, frontZ] }, pl);
        if (fences.some((s) => obbOverlap(opening, footprintObb(s, catalog), -1e-6))) {
          f.push({ msg: `${label} gate opening overlaps a fence segment`, a: g.pos });
        }
      } else f.push({ msg: `${label} gate has invalid width`, a: g.pos });
    }
    add('gate-at-door', f);
  }

  // house-facing — derive direction independently of the recorded diagnostics.
  {
    const f = [];
    const limits = applyDefaults(spec).houses.facing;
    const angle = (a, b) => Math.atan2(Math.abs(a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0]), a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1]) * 180 / Math.PI;
    for (const h of buildings) {
      const e = catalog.get(h.id);
      const localDir = e.door?.facing ?? e.pathSnaps?.[0]?.dir;
      const label = `instance ${inst.indexOf(h)} ${h.id}`;
      if (!localDir || !town.camera?.pos || !Number.isFinite(h.rotY)) {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} cannot compute facing: missing door direction, rotation or camera (cameraAngle=NaN plazaAngle=NaN)`, a: h.pos });
        continue;
      }
      const dir = rotDir([localDir[0], localDir[2]], h.rotY);
      const cameraAngle = angle(dir, [town.camera.pos[0] - h.pos[0], town.camera.pos[2] - h.pos[1]]);
      const plazaAngle = angle(dir, [-h.pos[0], -h.pos[1]]);
      const angles = `cameraAngle=${cameraAngle.toFixed(2)} plazaAngle=${plazaAngle.toFixed(2)}`;
      if (!(cameraAngle <= limits.maxCameraAngle && plazaAngle <= limits.maxPlazaAngle)) {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} ${angles} exceeds limits ${limits.maxCameraAngle}/${limits.maxPlazaAngle}`, a: h.pos });
      }
      if (h.facing && ['cameraAngle', 'plazaAngle'].some((key) => !Number.isFinite(h.facing[key]) || Math.abs(h.facing[key] - ({ cameraAngle, plazaAngle })[key]) > 2)) {
        f.push({ msg: `${label} facing data drift: ${angles}, recorded ${h.facing.cameraAngle}/${h.facing.plazaAngle}`, a: h.pos });
      }
    }
    add('house-facing', f);
  }

  // 11. house-count-in-range (density-scaled range).
  {
    const [lo, hi] = scaledRange(spec.houses.count, spec.density.houses);
    const f = (houses.length >= lo && houses.length <= hi) ? [] : [{ msg: `house count ${houses.length} outside [${lo},${hi}]`, a: [0, 0] }];
    add('house-count-in-range', f);
  }

  // 12. framing (warn).
  {
    const f = framingFailures(town, catalog);
    add('framing', f, true);
  }

  const ok = checks.every((c) => c.warn || c.ok);
  const stats = {
    houses: houses.length, landmarks: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'landmark').length,
    paths: paths.length, fences: fences.length,
    trees: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'tree').length,
    bushes: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'bush').length,
    props: inst.filter((i) => i.kind === 'prop').length,
    instances: inst.length,
  };
  return { ok, checks, stats };
}

function scaledRange([lo, hi], mult = 1) {
  const m = mult == null ? 1 : mult;
  const slo = Math.max(0, Math.round(lo * m));
  return [slo, Math.max(slo, Math.round(hi * m))];
}

/** Largest uncovered gap inside [tol, L-tol] given covered intervals. */
function maxGap(intervals, L, tol = 0) {
  if (L <= 0) return 0;
  const iv = intervals.map(([a, b]) => [Math.max(0, a), Math.min(L, b)]).filter(([a, b]) => b > a).sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0]);
  let cursor = tol, worst = 0;
  for (const [a, b] of iv) {
    if (a > cursor) worst = Math.max(worst, a - cursor);
    cursor = Math.max(cursor, b);
  }
  if (L - tol > cursor) worst = Math.max(worst, L - tol - cursor);
  return worst;
}

// Pure-math frustum check honouring the panel-reserved left region.
function framingFailures(town, catalog) {
  const cam = town.camera;
  if (!cam) return [];
  const eye = cam.pos, tgt = cam.target;
  const fwd = norm3([tgt[0] - eye[0], tgt[1] - eye[1], tgt[2] - eye[2]]);
  const right = norm3(cross(fwd, [0, 1, 0]));
  const up = cross(right, fwd);
  const halfV = (cam.fov || 42) * Math.PI / 360;
  const halfH = Math.atan(Math.tan(halfV) * 16 / 9);
  const tanH = Math.tan(halfH), tanV = Math.tan(halfV);
  const leftLim = -(1 - 2 * (cam.leftCut ?? 0)) - 0.02;
  const f = [];
  for (const i of town.instances) {
    if (!CORE_KINDS.has(i.kind) || (i.id !== '__plaza' && !catalog.get(i.id))) continue;
    const e = catalog.get(i.id);
    const rect = rectForInst(i, catalog);
    const yTop = (i.kind === 'ring') ? 0.1 : (e?.bounds?.max?.[1] ?? 0);
    for (const c of obbCorners(obbFromRect(rect, { x: i.pos[0], z: i.pos[1], rotY: i.rotY }, i.scale || 1))) {
      for (const y of [0, yTop]) {
        const v = [c[0] - eye[0], y - eye[1], c[1] - eye[2]];
        const zc = dot3(v, fwd); if (zc <= 0) { f.push({ msg: `${i.id} behind camera`, a: i.pos }); break; }
        const nx = dot3(v, right) / zc / tanH, ny = dot3(v, up) / zc / tanV;
        if (nx < leftLim || nx > 1.02 || Math.abs(ny) > 1.02) { f.push({ msg: `${i.id} outside frame`, a: i.pos }); break; }
      }
    }
  }
  return f;
}

const dot3 = (a, b) => a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2];
const cross = (a, b) => [a[1] * b[2] - a[2] * b[1], a[2] * b[0] - a[0] * b[2], a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0]];
function norm3(v) { const l = Math.hypot(v[0], v[1], v[2]) || 1; return [v[0] / l, v[1] / l, v[2] / l]; }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ground, dirt paths, rolling hills, sky dome, clouds.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function makeGround() {
  const g = new THREE.Group();

  const disk = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.CircleGeometry(70, 48),
    toonMaterial(PALETTE.grass)
  );
  disk.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  disk.receiveShadow = true;
  g.add(disk);

  // Central green: a slightly brighter raised patch
  const green = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(9, 40), toonMaterial(PALETTE.grassDark));
  green.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  green.position.y = 0.015;
  green.receiveShadow = true;
  g.add(green);

  return g;
}

// A flat dirt path quad from A to B.
export function makePath(x1, z1, x2, z2, width = 2.4) {
  const dx = x2 - x1;
  const dz = z2 - z1;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dz) + width * 0.5; // slight overlap at the ends
  const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, len);
  geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); // bake flat: length now runs along the local Z axis
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, toonMaterial(PALETTE.dirt));
  mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz); // orient length along the A->B line
    g.add(s);
  }
  return g;
}

export function makeHills() {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const spots = [
    [-45, -8, -55, 18, PALETTE.hillFar],
    [40, -6, -60, 22, PALETTE.hill],
    [0, -10, -70, 30, PALETTE.hillFar],
    [-62, -7, -30, 16, PALETTE.hill],
    [60, -9, -20, 20, PALETTE.hillFar],
    [-30, -6, -48, 14, PALETTE.hill],
  ];
  for (const [x, y, z, r, c] of spots) {
    const hill = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, 20, 14), toonMaterial(c));
    hill.position.set(x, y, z);
    hill.scale.y = 0.5;
    hill.receiveShadow = true;
    g.add(hill);
  }
  return g;
}

// Main page. Default: the hand-placed hamlet. With `/?seed=N`: compile + render
// that seed through the compiler runtime (same layout engine as compile.html)
// behind the plain HUD. Both paths set window.__ready for headless screenshots.
const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
if (q.has('seed')) runCompiled(Number(q.get('seed')));
else runHamlet();

// --- compiled seed through the runtime -------------------------------------
async function runCompiled(seed) {
  const spec = validateSpec(HAMLET, RESOLVED);
  const town = compileTown(spec, RESOLVED, { seed: Number.isFinite(seed) ? seed : undefined });
  const cam = town.camera;
  const stage = createStage({
    camPos: cam.pos, target: cam.target, minDistance: 8, maxDistance: 220, shadowSize: 48, fog: [120, 320],
    fitRadius: cam.radius + 4,
  });
  const { renderer, scene } = stage;
  await buildTown(town, scene);
  // No left panel here: frame the built core centred and tight.
  const coreBoxes = townCoreBoxes(scene);
  stage.setIsoFit(coreBoxes, { panelFrac: 0.0 });
  stage.setPerspectiveFit(coreBoxes, { panelFrac: 0.0, fill: 0.86 });

  const hud = document.getElementById('hud');
  if (hud) {
    hud.innerHTML = `<b>Cel&nbsp;Town</b> &nbsp;·&nbsp; <span>seed ${town.seed} · `
      + `${town.ok ? 'OK' : 'best'} · ${town.report.stats.houses}h ${town.report.stats.landmarks}L · drag to orbit</span>`;
  }

  const post = createPost(renderer, scene, stage.camera, parseOptions());
  post.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
  post.setHullVisible(post.options.hull);
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => post.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight));
  createLookPanel({ post, stage });

  let ready = false;
  (function loop() {
    stage.controls.update();
    post.render(stage.camera);
    if (!ready) { ready = true; window.__ready = true; maybeMeasurePerf(renderer, post, () => stage.camera); }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  })();
}

// --- hand-placed hamlet (the default view) ---------------------------------
function runHamlet() {
  const stage = createStage({ fitRadius: 34 });
  const { renderer, scene } = stage;

  scene.add(makeSky());
// 2D geometry on the XZ ground plane for the compiler and checks. Pure JS, no
// three.js. Points are [x, z]; a "rect" is a local-frame AABB { min:[x,z],
// max:[x,z] } (as resolved footprint / lot / bounds-XZ); an OBB is that rect
// rotated+translated by a placement { x, z, rotY }.

import { math as Math } from './math.js';

export const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

/** Local [x,z] -> world [x,z] under a placement. Matches schema.toWorld's XZ. */
export function toWorldXZ([lx, lz], { x = 0, z = 0, rotY = 0 }) {
  const c = Math.cos(rotY), s = Math.sin(rotY);
  return [x + lx * c + lz * s, z - lx * s + lz * c];
}

/** Rotate a local direction [x,z] by rotY (no translation). */
export function rotDir([dx, dz], rotY) {
  const c = Math.cos(rotY), s = Math.sin(rotY);
  return [dx * c + dz * s, -dx * s + dz * c];
}

/** OBB from a local rect + placement, with an optional extra uniform scale. */
export function obbFromRect(rect, place, scale = 1) {
  const cx = ((rect.min[0] + rect.max[0]) / 2) * scale;
  const cz = ((rect.min[1] + rect.max[1]) / 2) * scale;
  const hu = ((rect.max[0] - rect.min[0]) / 2) * scale;
  const hv = ((rect.max[1] - rect.min[1]) / 2) * scale;
  const [wx, wz] = toWorldXZ([cx, cz], place);
  return {
    c: [wx, wz],
    u: rotDir([1, 0], place.rotY || 0), // world axis for local X
    v: rotDir([0, 1], place.rotY || 0), // world axis for local Z
    hu, hv,
  };
}

/** XZ rect from a 3D bounds { min:[x,y,z], max:[x,y,z] }. */
export const rectXZ = (b) => ({ min: [b.min[0], b.min[2]], max: [b.max[0], b.max[2]] });

export function obbCorners(o) {
  const out = [];
  for (const su of [-1, 1]) for (const sv of [-1, 1]) {
    out.push([
      o.c[0] + su * o.hu * o.u[0] + sv * o.hv * o.v[0],
      o.c[1] + su * o.hu * o.u[1] + sv * o.hv * o.v[1],
    ]);
  }
  return out;
}

function projRadius(o, ax) {
  return Math.abs(o.u[0] * ax[0] + o.u[1] * ax[1]) * o.hu
       + Math.abs(o.v[0] * ax[0] + o.v[1] * ax[1]) * o.hv;
}

/** SAT overlap of two OBBs; `gap` inflates both (require that much clearance). */
export function obbOverlap(a, b, gap = 0) {
  for (const ax of [a.u, a.v, b.u, b.v]) {
    const d = Math.abs((b.c[0] - a.c[0]) * ax[0] + (b.c[1] - a.c[1]) * ax[1]);
    if (d - projRadius(a, ax) - projRadius(b, ax) - gap >= 0) return false; // separated
  }
  return true;
}

/** Signed separation of two OBBs: >=0 apart (max axis gap), <0 overlapping. */
export function obbSeparation(a, b) {
  let maxGap = -Infinity;
  for (const ax of [a.u, a.v, b.u, b.v]) {
    const d = Math.abs((b.c[0] - a.c[0]) * ax[0] + (b.c[1] - a.c[1]) * ax[1]);
    const gap = d - projRadius(a, ax) - projRadius(b, ax);
    if (gap > maxGap) maxGap = gap;
  }
  return maxGap;
}

/** Distance from a point to an OBB (0 if inside). */
export function obbPointDistance(o, p) {
  const dx = p[0] - o.c[0], dz = p[1] - o.c[1];
  const lu = Math.abs(dx * o.u[0] + dz * o.u[1]) - o.hu;
  const lv = Math.abs(dx * o.v[0] + dz * o.v[1]) - o.hv;
  return Math.hypot(Math.max(lu, 0), Math.max(lv, 0));
}

export const obbContainsPoint = (o, p) => obbPointDistance(o, p) === 0;

/** Distance from point p to segment a-b. */
export function segPointDistance(a, b, p) {
  const abx = b[0] - a[0], abz = b[1] - a[1];
  const len2 = abx * abx + abz * abz || 1e-9;
  let t = ((p[0] - a[0]) * abx + (p[1] - a[1]) * abz) / len2;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  return Math.hypot(p[0] - (a[0] + abx * t), p[1] - (a[1] + abz * t));
}

export const dist = (a, b) => Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]);
export function norm([x, z]) { const l = Math.hypot(x, z) || 1; return [x / l, z / l]; }

/** Is a point inside the area, shrunk by `margin`? circle or rect. */
export function pointInArea(area, [x, z], margin = 0) {
  if (area.shape === 'rect') return Math.abs(x) <= area.w / 2 - margin && Math.abs(z) <= area.d / 2 - margin;
  return Math.hypot(x, z) <= area.radius - margin;
}

/** Centreline segment endpoints [A, B] of a path strip (length along local Z). */
export function strutEnds(p) {
  const { pos, rotY } = p;
  const length = p.length ?? p.params?.length ?? 0;
  const d = rotDir([0, 1], rotY);
  return [
    [pos[0] - (d[0] * length) / 2, pos[1] - (d[1] * length) / 2],
    [pos[0] + (d[0] * length) / 2, pos[1] + (d[1] * length) / 2],
  ];
}

  if (_matCache.has(key)) return _matCache.get(key);
  const mat = new THREE.MeshToonMaterial({
    color: new THREE.Color(color),
    gradientMap: RAMPS[ramp] || RAMP,
  });
  _matCache.set(key, mat);
  return mat;
}

// Inverted-hull outline: a back-faced copy of the geometry pushed outward
// along its normals, drawn in ink. Stable, clean, camera-independent.
const _outlineCache = new Map();
// Hull ink. The shader used to write the linear colour straight to the
// screen, which only looked right by accident (near-black on screen, but the
// true #20262b once rendered through the post chain's linear targets). Now it
// encodes like every other material, so both paths draw the same ink.
const OUTLINE_INK = '#0e1114';
// Distance fade for every hull, shared by all outline materials. The post
// chain sets uFadeDist each frame to the view depth of the town core
// (0 = fading off, e.g. ?inkFade=0 or ortho). In perspective the hull keeps
// full thickness up to that depth and thins linearly to uFadeMin at 2x it, so
// far fences and trees don't turn into solid ink.
export const HULL_FADE = { uFadeDist: { value: 0 }, uFadeMin: { value: 0.35 } };
export function outlineMaterial(thickness) {
  const key = thickness.toFixed(4);
  if (_outlineCache.has(key)) return _outlineCache.get(key);
  const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    uniforms: { uThickness: { value: thickness }, uInk: { value: new THREE.Color(OUTLINE_INK) }, ...HULL_FADE },
    vertexShader: /* glsl */ `
      uniform float uThickness, uFadeDist, uFadeMin;
      void main() {
        float k = 1.0;
        // projectionMatrix[2][3] is -1 for perspective, 0 for ortho.
        if (uFadeDist > 0.0 && projectionMatrix[2][3] != 0.0) {
          float depth = -(modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0)).z;
          k = mix(1.0, uFadeMin, clamp((depth - uFadeDist) / uFadeDist, 0.0, 1.0));
        }
        vec3 p = position + normalize(normal) * uThickness * k;
        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(p, 1.0);
      }
    `,
    fragmentShader: /* glsl */ `
      uniform vec3 uInk;
      void main() {
        gl_FragColor = vec4(uInk, 1.0);
        #include <colorspace_fragment>
      }
    `,
    side: THREE.BackSide,
  });
  _outlineCache.set(key, mat);
  return mat;
}

// Build a toon-shaded mesh (+ optional ink outline) as a small Group.
export function toon(geometry, color, opts = {}) {
  const { outline = true, thickness = 0.04, cast = true, receive = true, ramp = 'default' } = opts;
  const group = new THREE.Group();

  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, toonMaterial(color, { ramp }));
  mesh.castShadow = cast;
  mesh.receiveShadow = receive;
  // Foliage flag: the post prepass masks crease ink inside tree/bush crowns.
  if (ramp === 'foliage') mesh.userData.foliage = true;
  group.add(mesh);

  if (outline) {
    const edge = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, outlineMaterial(thickness));
    edge.castShadow = false;
    edge.receiveShadow = false;
    edge.userData.outline = true; // post passes hide hulls via this flag
    group.add(edge);
  }
  return group;
}

// Toon material for imported vertex-coloured meshes (e.g. Cozy Village). The
// glTF base colour is white, so the vertex colours carry the palette.
const _vcMat = {};
export function toonVertexColorMaterial({ ramp = 'default' } = {}) {
  if (_vcMat[ramp]) return _vcMat[ramp];
  _vcMat[ramp] = new THREE.MeshToonMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, vertexColors: true, gradientMap: RAMPS[ramp] || RAMP });
  return _vcMat[ramp];
}

export function townCoreBoxes(scene) {
  const out = [];
  scene.traverse((o) => {
    const inst = o.userData.inst;
    if (!inst || o.userData.outline) return;
    if (inst.kind === 'tree' || inst.kind === 'bush') return;
    // precise: vertex-exact bounds, so rotated buildings aren't inflated.
    const b = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(o, true);
    if (!b.isEmpty()) out.push(b);
  });
  return out;
}
  // View depth of the town core: where the view axis meets the ground. 0 when
  // fading is off (ortho, ?inkFade=0, or looking at the sky).
  function fadeDistance(cam) {
    if (!options.inkFade || cam.isOrthographicCamera) return 0;
    cam.getWorldDirection(_fwd);
    if (_fwd.y > -0.02) return 0;
    return Math.min(cam.far * 0.5, Math.max(5, cam.position.y / -_fwd.y));
  }

  function texel(mat) { mat.uniforms.uTexel && mat.uniforms.uTexel.value.set(1 / size.x, 1 / size.y); }

  // Run a fullscreen pass src->dst (dst null = screen). Ping-pongs rtA/rtB.
  let last = null;
  function pass(mat, src, toScreen) {
    mat.uniforms.tDiffuse.value = src;
    const dst = toScreen ? null : (last === rtA ? rtB : rtA);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(dst);
    quad.material = mat;
    quad.render(renderer);
    if (!toScreen) last = dst;
    return dst && dst.texture;
  }

  function render(cam = camera) {
    const mask = cam.layers.mask;
    const fadeDist = fadeDistance(cam);
    HULL_FADE.uFadeDist.value = fadeDist;
    HULL_FADE.uFadeMin.value = FADE_MIN;
    if (!options.post) {
      cam.layers.enable(0); cam.layers.enable(HULL_LAYER);
      renderer.setRenderTarget(null); renderer.render(scene, cam);
      cam.layers.mask = mask;
      return;
    }
    last = null;

    // Everything but the hulls.
    cam.layers.set(0);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(sceneRT);
    renderer.render(scene, cam);

    const wantPrepass = options.edges || options.normals;
    if (wantPrepass) {
      scene.overrideMaterial = normalMat;
      renderer.setRenderTarget(prepassRT);
      renderer.render(scene, cam);
      if (options.foliageMask && options.normals) {
        // Redraw only the foliage (equal depth passes LessEqual) with alpha 0.5.
        const auto = renderer.autoClear;
        const bg = scene.background; scene.background = null; // a Color bg would force a clear
        renderer.autoClear = false;
        scene.overrideMaterial = foliageNormalMat;
        cam.layers.set(FOLIAGE_LAYER);
        renderer.render(scene, cam);
        cam.layers.set(0);
        renderer.autoClear = auto;
        scene.background = bg;
      }
      scene.overrideMaterial = null;
    }

// Computes bounds for every catalog entry from real geometry and writes
// src/catalog/bounds.json (derived numbers only — no licensed geometry).
//   meshes:   glTF-Transform + Draco decoder over the GLB in public/
//   builders: construct the builder under three.js in node, walk its meshes
// All numbers are SOURCE units, before scale / yOffset. Run: npm run catalog:bounds
import { NodeIO } from '@gltf-transform/core';
import { ALL_EXTENSIONS } from '@gltf-transform/extensions';
import draco3d from 'draco3dgltf';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { CATALOG } from '../src/catalog/catalog.js';
import { BUILDERS } from '../src/catalog/builders-registry.js';
import { validateEntry } from '../src/catalog/schema.js';

const io = new NodeIO().registerExtensions(ALL_EXTENSIONS)
  .registerDependencies({ 'draco3d.decoder': await draco3d.createDecoderModule() });

async function meshPoints(e) {
  const file = new URL('../public/' + e.path, import.meta.url).pathname;
  if (!existsSync(file)) return null;
  const doc = await io.read(file);
  const pts = []; let tris = 0;
  for (const node of doc.getRoot().listNodes()) {
    const mesh = node.getMesh(); if (!mesh) continue;
    const M = new THREE.Matrix4().fromArray(node.getWorldMatrix());
    for (const prim of mesh.listPrimitives()) {
      const pos = prim.getAttribute('POSITION'); const idx = prim.getIndices();
      tris += (idx ? idx.getCount() : pos.getCount()) / 3;
      const v = [0, 0, 0];
      for (let i = 0; i < pos.getCount(); i++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(...pos.getElement(i, v)).applyMatrix4(M));
    }
  }
  return { pts, tris };
}

function builderPoints(e) {
  const obj = BUILDERS[e.builder](e.params || {});
  obj.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const pts = []; let tris = 0;
  obj.traverse((o) => {
    if (!o.isMesh || o.material.isShaderMaterial) return; // skip outline hulls
    const pos = o.geometry.attributes.position;
    tris += (o.geometry.index ? o.geometry.index.count : pos.count) / 3;
    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(pos, i).applyMatrix4(o.matrixWorld));
  });
  return { pts, tris };
}

const out = {};
for (const e of CATALOG) {
  validateEntry(e);
  const r = e.source === 'mesh' ? await meshPoints(e) : builderPoints(e);
  if (!r) { console.warn('MISSING', e.id, e.path); continue; }
  const { pts, tris } = r;
  const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(pts);
  const ground = pts.filter((p) => p.y < box.min.y + (e.groundY ?? 0.6));
  const g = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(ground);
  let roof = null;
  if (e.eaveY != null) {
    const rb = new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(pts.filter((p) => p.y >= e.eaveY));
    roof = { min: rb.min.toArray(), max: rb.max.toArray() };
  }
  const r3 = (a) => a.map((v) => Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000);
  out[e.id] = {
    tris: Math.round(tris),
    min: r3(box.min.toArray()), max: r3(box.max.toArray()),
    base: { min: r3([g.min.x, g.min.z]), max: r3([g.max.x, g.max.z]) },
    roof: roof && { min: r3(roof.min), max: r3(roof.max) },
  };
  const size = box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).multiplyScalar(e.scale);
  console.log(e.id.padEnd(20), String(Math.round(tris)).padStart(5), 'tris  world size', size.toArray().map((v) => v.toFixed(2)).join(' x '));
}
writeFileSync(new URL('../src/catalog/bounds.json', import.meta.url), JSON.stringify(out, null, 1) + '\n');
console.log('wrote src/catalog/bounds.json');

  name: file.replaceAll('"'_', ' '), tags: { category, style: [pack, ...styles] },
  source: 'mesh', path: "'`licensed/${pack}/${file}.glb`, license: '"'tja-commercial',
  scale: pack === 'cozy-village' ? 1.6 : 1, groundY: 100, eaveY: null, door: null,
});
function building(pack, file, category, wall, step, eave) {
  const e = mesh(pack, file, category, [category === 'house' ? 'dwelling' : 'civic']);
  return { ...e, groundY: 0.4, eaveY: eave, lotMargin: 1,
    door: { pos: [0, 0, wall], facing: [0, 0, 1] },
    pathSnaps: [{ pos: [0, 0, step + 0.3], dir: [0, 0, 1] }] };
}
